----- BUILD BRIEF -----
# Build brief: L1 parity for browser-direct Binance (`build/browser-direct-l1`)

2026-10-26. Browser-direct mode had no frame the runtime *derives* from the
tape (`marker`, the aggressive pool, `aggressive_history`, `marker_history`), so
Large Trade Detector drew its legend and no bubbles. This build computes them in
the worker with the runtime's own algorithms, proven equal by parity tests that
`require` the runtime's CommonJS modules on the same 10,000 recorded trades.

## 1. What the runtime derives from trades, and who reads it

There is no "baseline" for Binance markers (`baseline_log_*` are dxFeed storage
columns). `history.js` retains, per minute and side, the four prints of largest
**USD notional**, ties by time then id, 100,000 at most, seven days; a `marker`
follows the admitted `trade`; the snapshot carries the newest 5,000;
`marker_history` (opt-in) the full set, 5,000 rows a frame. The pool keeps
prints at or above a floor (98th percentile of the last 4,096 sizes once 200
are seen, never falling), 4 hours, 20,000 at most, on the snapshot as
`aggressive_prints*`; `aggressive_history` answers from storage, 10,000 a page.

| L1 indicator | Reads | Browser-direct before | Added by this build |
|---|---|---|---|
| Aggressive Order Flow, Delta Divergence, CVD Divergence, Market Structure Delta, FVG Delta, FVG Boxes, L1 Order Blocks, Volume Profile OHLC | bars via `barSides` | works | nothing |
| Large Trade Detector | `markers` (snapshot, `marker`, `marker_history`) and bar `trade_count` | legend only | live markers; bootstrap markers; `marker_history` |
| VWAP Bands, Large Trade Bubbles (tick store) | `ctx.ticks` / `ctx.trades`: live tape plus `aggressive_prints` and `aggressive_history` | live bars only (`coverageOf` "none" before connect) | pool in the snapshot; `aggressive_history` from the pool |
| Live Delta Profile, Volume Profile (Ticks) | live trades plus `delta_profile` (every trade, per price row) | forming window only | nothing: the pool is floor-filtered; reply stays `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE` |
| VPIN Cloud | `vpin` frames (server study) | unavailable | nothing: `vpin_status: "DISABLED"` |

## 2. Shared algorithm: approach (a)

The runtime modules are CommonJS and the Vite build cannot import
`apps/runtime`. Pure TypeScript mirrors live in `apps/web/src/lib/tape/`:
`largeTradeDetection.ts` (line for line with `large-trade-detection.js`) and
`aggressivePool.ts` (`createAggressivePool`, `print-floor.js`, `floorAbove`).
Marker retention reuses `lib/chart/markerRetention.ts`, already the browser
mirror of `history.js`'s time-covered path. `workers/tests/tapeParity.test.ts`
`require`s the runtime files (vitest runs in Node) and asserts identical
output on 10,000 recorded BTC-USDT aggregate trades: every helper result,
every admit decision, the marker set, the pool's prints, floor and `from`.
Runtime code is not touched.

## 3. Markers

Every live `trade` goes through the marker buffer; an admitted print goes out
as a `marker` right after it. Bootstrap: after the first snapshot, alongside
the stream, the worker pages `GET /api/v3/aggTrades?limit=1000` from the
mirror, newest first by `fromId`, at most 30 pages (weight 4 each, 120 of the
6,000 per minute budget, one 10 s timeout per page), stopping at a short page
or seven days. Rows are aggregate trades: id `a<aggTradeId>` (a separate id space
from the stream's `t`), size the summed fills of one taker order at one price,
so a bootstrap marker can be larger than any single print the runtime saw.
Then the `snapshot` is re-broadcast (closed bars, last 100 live trades, newest
5,000 markers, the pool), as the runtime does after a warm-up chunk, and the
forming bar re-sent. The runtime's baseline is seven days of storage; the
browser's is what 30 pages cover (about twenty minutes on BTC-USDT, hours on
a quiet pair). The detector's `top N%` target counts the snapshot bars' `trade_count`
(15 hours), so a short bootstrap leaves the selection `truncated`; the legend
appends ` · partial`.

## 4. Aggressive pool and `aggressive_history`

The pool is the runtime's: live trades `record`; bootstrap pages are staged
until the stream's first trade is known (or a 2 s grace), reconciled against
every live span by fill id (`f`/`l`), then `seed`, the newest 15 minutes with
`calibrate: true`, older rows at the floor. Coverage is explicit: the
transport keeps every stretch of tape observed in full (validated
id-contiguous pages, each stream span first-to-last trade, ids at the ends)
and claims only inside one, minus milliseconds whose aggregates straddle a
span boundary; the REST-to-stream seam and reconnect holes are filled from
`aggTrades` (by id after a REST row, else `startTime`, 10 pages, three tries)
or stay holes; the bookkeeping is pruned to the pool's 4 h and bounded. A
stream lost after trades closes the transport, as a lost runtime socket
would: the worker reports `closed` (the tick store ends its live stretch,
`AggressiveTape.endLive`) and reconnects to a new instance that keeps the
pair's tape state. `aggressive_prints_from` is the start of the stretch
reaching now, or now itself (no claim) while no stream delivers. A page with
a malformed row is a failed read, never a shorter page. `aggressive_history` is answered from the pool: `complete: true`
when the window lies inside one observed stretch and `min_size >= floor`,
trades in time order, 10,000 a page with `CAP_REACHED` and a base64url cursor (`{ts, id}` bound to the
request); otherwise `complete: false`, `reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE"`,
`trades: []`, which the client retries three times and then rests, as today.

## 5. `marker_history`

With the `capabilities` flag set (the worker always sends it), the bootstrap's
completion, a later opt-in and a connect with kept state each send the full
marker set as `marker_history` frames, `reset` on the first, 5,000 rows each.

## 6. Degraded states

A failed first page is retried with the stream's backoff; after three failures
the READY health frame carries `reason: "BROWSER_DIRECT_NO_TAPE_HISTORY"`,
markers and the pool start at connect, retries continue (a later success
publishes the snapshot and restores `BROWSER_DIRECT`). A failure after some
pages keeps the pages validated before it.

## 7. Unavailable, with reason

`delta_profile`: `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE` (needs every trade; the pool is floor
filtered). VPIN: `vpin_status: "DISABLED"`.

+  /**
+   * The feed is gone (the worker reported its socket closed): the live
+   * stretch ends at its last print and nothing is live until the next print
+   * or snapshot starts a new stretch, so a window that spans the outage
+   * never reads as complete, not even before the reconnect's snapshot.
+   */
+  endLive(): void {
+    if (this._liveSince === null) return;
+    if (this._lastLiveTs > this._liveSince) {
+      this._liveSpans.push({ from: this._liveSince, to: this._lastLiveTs });
+      if (this._liveSpans.length > MAX_LIVE_SPANS) this._liveSpans.splice(0, this._liveSpans.length - MAX_LIVE_SPANS);
+    }
+    this._liveSince = null;
+    this._notify();
+  }

+// Browser mirror of apps/runtime/src/server/aggressive-pool.js (createAggressivePool),
+// with its two helpers from apps/runtime/src/indicators/passive_liquidity/
+// print-floor.js (createPrintFloor) and bounded-prints.js (floorAbove), each
+// line for line. The pool is the server's bounded store of big prints: a size
+// floor from the 98th percentile of the last 4,096 sizes once 200 are seen,
+// never falling; four hours deep; 20,000 prints at most, trimmed by size with
+// the floor raised to what survived, so what it reports is complete from its
+// floor over [from, now]. The browser-direct transport runs it in the worker;
+// workers/tests/tapeParity.test.ts requires the runtime file and asserts the
+// two agree print for print. Change both or neither.
+
+export interface PoolPrint {
+  readonly ts_exchange: number;
+  readonly size: number;
+  readonly trade_id: string;
+}
+
+export interface PrintFloor {
+  push(size: number): void;
+  value(): number;
+}
+
+export interface PoolSnapshot<T extends PoolPrint> {
+  readonly prints: readonly T[];
+  readonly floor: number;
+  readonly from: number;
+}
+
+export interface AggressivePool<T extends PoolPrint> {
+  record(trade: T): boolean;
+  seed(trades: readonly T[], options?: { fromMs?: number; calibrate?: boolean }): number;
+  snapshot(): PoolSnapshot<T>;
+  gate(): number;
+  readonly size: number;
+  readonly floor: number;
+}
+
+export const DEFAULT_WINDOW_MS = 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
+export const DEFAULT_MAX_PRINTS = 20_000;
+export const DEFAULT_FLOOR_SHARE = 0.02;
+export const DEFAULT_FLOOR_WINDOW = 4_096;
+export const DEFAULT_FLOOR_MIN_SAMPLES = 200;
+export const DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
+
+export function floorAbove(size: number): number {
+  return size + Number.EPSILON * Math.max(1, size);
+}
+
+export function createPrintFloor({ window, share, minSamples }: { window: number; share: number; minSamples: number }): PrintFloor {
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(window) || window <= 0) throw new Error("print-floor: window must be a positive safe integer");
+  if (!(share > 0 && share < 1)) throw new Error("print-floor: share must be in (0, 1)");
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(minSamples) || minSamples <= 0) throw new Error("print-floor: minSamples must be a positive safe integer");
+
+  const arrivals = new Float64Array(window);
+  const sorted = new Float64Array(window);
+  let next = 0;
+  let filled = 0;
+
+  function firstAbove(size: number): number {
+    let lo = 0;
+    let hi = filled;
+    while (lo < hi) {
+      const mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
+      if (sorted[mid] <= size) lo = mid + 1;
+      else hi = mid;
+    }
+    return lo;
+  }
+
+  function remove(size: number): void {
+    const at = firstAbove(size) - 1;
+    sorted.copyWithin(at, at + 1, filled);
+    filled -= 1;
+  }
+
+  function insert(size: number): void {
+    const at = firstAbove(size);
+    sorted.copyWithin(at + 1, at, filled);
+    sorted[at] = size;
+    filled += 1;
+  }
+
+  function push(size: number): void {
+    if (filled === window) remove(arrivals[next]);
+    arrivals[next] = size;
+    next = (next + 1) % window;
+    insert(size);
+  }
+
+  function value(): number {
+    if (filled < minSamples) return 0;
+    const index = Math.min(filled - 1, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(filled * (1 - share)) - 1));
+    return sorted[index];
+  }
+
+  return Object.freeze({ push, value });
+}
+
+function assertPositiveInteger(value: number, field: string): void {
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0) throw new Error(`aggressive-pool: ${field} must be a positive safe integer`);
+}
+
+export function createAggressivePool<T extends PoolPrint>({
+  windowMs = DEFAULT_WINDOW_MS,
+  maxPrints = DEFAULT_MAX_PRINTS,
+  floorShare = DEFAULT_FLOOR_SHARE,
+  floorWindow = DEFAULT_FLOOR_WINDOW,
+  floorMinSamples = DEFAULT_FLOOR_MIN_SAMPLES,
+  now = () => Date.now(),
+}: {
+  windowMs?: number;
+  maxPrints?: number;
+  floorShare?: number;
+  floorWindow?: number;
+  floorMinSamples?: number;
+  now?: () => number;
+} = {}): AggressivePool<T> {
+  assertPositiveInteger(windowMs, "windowMs");
+  assertPositiveInteger(maxPrints, "maxPrints");
+  assertPositiveInteger(floorWindow, "floorWindow");
+  assertPositiveInteger(floorMinSamples, "floorMinSamples");
+  if (!(floorShare > 0 && floorShare < 1)) throw new Error("aggressive-pool: floorShare must be in (0, 1)");
+
+  const printFloor = createPrintFloor({ window: floorWindow, share: floorShare, minSamples: floorMinSamples });
+  let prints: T[] = [];
+  const ids = new Set<string>();
+  let coverageFloor = 0;
+  let from: number | null = null;
+
+  function gate(): number {
+    return printFloor.value();
+  }
+
+  function insert(trade: T): boolean {
+    if (ids.has(trade.trade_id)) return false;
+    ids.add(trade.trade_id);
+    const last = prints[prints.length - 1];
+    if (!last || last.ts_exchange <= trade.ts_exchange) {
+      prints.push(trade);
+      return true;
+    }
+    let lo = 0;
+    let hi = prints.length;
+    while (lo < hi) {
+      const mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
+      if (prints[mid].ts_exchange <= trade.ts_exchange) lo = mid + 1;
+      else hi = mid;
+    }
+    prints.splice(lo, 0, trade);
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  function trimWindow(at: number): void {
+    const cutoff = at - windowMs;
+    let drop = 0;
+    while (drop < prints.length && prints[drop].ts_exchange < cutoff) drop += 1;
+    if (drop > 0) for (const gone of prints.splice(0, drop)) ids.delete(gone.trade_id);
+    if (from === null || from < cutoff) from = cutoff;
+  }
+
+  function trimCount(): void {
+    if (prints.length <= maxPrints) return;
+    const bySize = prints.map((print) => print.size).sort((a, b) => a - b);
+    let threshold = bySize[prints.length - maxPrints];
+    let atOrAbove = 0;
+    for (const size of bySize) if (size >= threshold) atOrAbove += 1;
+    if (atOrAbove > maxPrints) {
+      const above = bySize.find((size) => size > threshold);
+      if (above === undefined) {
+        for (const gone of prints) ids.delete(gone.trade_id);
+        prints = [];
+        coverageFloor = Math.max(coverageFloor, floorAbove(threshold));
+        return;
+      }
+      threshold = above;
+    }
+    const kept: T[] = [];
+    for (const print of prints) {
+      if (print.size < threshold) ids.delete(print.trade_id);
+      else kept.push(print);
+    }
+    prints = kept;
+    if (threshold > coverageFloor) coverageFloor = threshold;
+  }
+
+  function record(trade: T): boolean {
+    if (!(trade.size > 0)) return false;
+    printFloor.push(trade.size);
+    const floor = gate();
+    if (floor > coverageFloor) coverageFloor = floor;
+    if (trade.size < Math.max(floor, coverageFloor)) return false;
+    if (!insert(trade)) return false;
+    trimWindow(now());
+    trimCount();
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  function mergeInsertAll(candidates: T[]): number {
+    if (candidates.length === 0) return 0;
+    const ordered = candidates.length === 1 ? candidates : [...candidates].sort((a, b) => a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange);
+    const merged: T[] = [];
+    let i = 0;
+    let j = 0;
+    while (i < prints.length && j < ordered.length) {
+      if (prints[i].ts_exchange <= ordered[j].ts_exchange) merged.push(prints[i++]);
+      else merged.push(ordered[j++]);
+    }
+    while (i < prints.length) merged.push(prints[i++]);
+    while (j < ordered.length) merged.push(ordered[j++]);
+    for (const trade of ordered) ids.add(trade.trade_id);
+    prints = merged;
+    return ordered.length;
+  }
+
+  function seed(trades: readonly T[], { fromMs, calibrate = false }: { fromMs?: number; calibrate?: boolean } = {}): number {
+    const admit = calibrate ? 0 : Math.max(coverageFloor, gate());
+    const candidates: T[] = [];
+    const seenInBatch = new Set<string>();
+    for (const trade of trades) {
+      if (!(trade.size > 0) || trade.size < admit) continue;
+      if (calibrate) printFloor.push(trade.size);
+      if (ids.has(trade.trade_id) || seenInBatch.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
+      seenInBatch.add(trade.trade_id);
+      candidates.push(trade);
+    }
+    const taken = mergeInsertAll(candidates);
+    if (calibrate) {
+      const floor = gate();
+      if (floor > coverageFloor) coverageFloor = floor;
+      if (floor > 0) {
+        const survivors = prints.filter((print) => print.size >= floor);
+        if (survivors.length !== prints.length) {
+          for (const print of prints) if (print.size < floor) ids.delete(print.trade_id);
+          prints = survivors;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    if (fromMs !== undefined && Number.isFinite(fromMs) && (from === null || fromMs < from)) from = fromMs;
+    trimWindow(now());
+    trimCount();
+    return taken;
+  }
+
+  function boundedForWire(): { prints: T[]; floor: number } {
+    const floor = Math.max(coverageFloor, gate());
+    if (prints.length <= maxPrints) return { prints: prints.slice(), floor };
+    const bySize = prints.slice().sort((a, b) => b.size - a.size);
+    let kept = 0;
+    while (kept < bySize.length) {
+      let end = kept;
+      while (end < bySize.length && bySize[end].size === bySize[kept].size) end += 1;
+      if (end > maxPrints) break;
+      kept = end;
+    }
+    if (kept === 0) {
+      return { prints: [], floor: Math.max(floor, floorAbove(bySize[0].size)) };
+    }
+    const keptIds = new Set(bySize.slice(0, kept).map((print) => print.trade_id));
+    return { prints: prints.filter((print) => keptIds.has(print.trade_id)), floor: Math.max(floor, bySize[kept - 1].size) };
+  }
+
+  function snapshot(): PoolSnapshot<T> {
+    trimWindow(now());
+    const bounded = boundedForWire();
+    return Object.freeze({ prints: Object.freeze(bounded.prints), floor: bounded.floor, from: from ?? now() });
+  }
+
+  return Object.freeze({
+    record,
+    seed,
+    snapshot,
+    gate,
+    get size() {
+      return prints.length;
+    },
+    get floor() {
+      return Math.max(coverageFloor, gate());
+    },
+  });
+}

+// Browser mirror of apps/runtime/src/indicators/large-trade/large-trade-detection.js,
+// line for line: the magnitude a print is ranked by (USD notional on Binance,
+// contracts on dxFeed futures), the tie-break order and the top-fraction count.
+// The runtime module is CommonJS and the Vite build cannot import it;
+// workers/tests/tapeParity.test.ts requires the runtime file and asserts the
+// two agree. Change both or neither.
+
+export const CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_PROVIDER = "DXFEED";
+export const CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_ASSET_CLASS = "FUTURE";
+
+export const MARKER_BUCKET_MS = 60_000;
+export const MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE = 4;
+
+export const MIN_TOP_FRACTION = 0.0002;
+export const MAX_TOP_FRACTION = 0.02;
+export const DEFAULT_TOP_FRACTION = 0.002;
+
+export interface RankedPrint {
+  readonly provider: string;
+  readonly assetClass?: string;
+  readonly price: number;
+  readonly size: number;
+  readonly ts_exchange: number;
+  readonly trade_id: string;
+}
+
+export interface TopFractionCount {
+  readonly target: number;
+  readonly shown: number;
+  readonly truncated: boolean;
+  readonly effectiveFraction: number;
+}
+
+export function usesContractMagnitude(provider: string, assetClass: string | undefined): boolean {
+  return provider === CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_PROVIDER && assetClass === CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_ASSET_CLASS;
+}
+
+export function tradeMagnitude(provider: string, assetClass: string | undefined, price: number, size: number): number {
+  return usesContractMagnitude(provider, assetClass) ? size : price * size;
+}
+
+export function markerMagnitude(markerFrame: RankedPrint): number {
+  return tradeMagnitude(markerFrame.provider, markerFrame.assetClass, markerFrame.price, markerFrame.size);
+}
+
+export function lowerMagnitudePriority(candidate: RankedPrint, incumbent: RankedPrint): boolean {
+  const candidateMagnitude = markerMagnitude(candidate);
+  const incumbentMagnitude = markerMagnitude(incumbent);
+  if (candidateMagnitude !== incumbentMagnitude) return candidateMagnitude < incumbentMagnitude;
+  if (candidate.ts_exchange !== incumbent.ts_exchange) return candidate.ts_exchange < incumbent.ts_exchange;
+  return candidate.trade_id < incumbent.trade_id;
+}
+
+export function resolveTopFractionCount(totalTradeCount: number, fraction: number, availableMarkerCount: number): TopFractionCount {
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(totalTradeCount) || totalTradeCount < 0) {
+    throw new Error("large-trade-detection: totalTradeCount must be a non-negative safe integer");
+  }
+  if (!Number.isFinite(fraction) || fraction <= 0 || fraction > 1) {
+    throw new Error("large-trade-detection: fraction must be a finite number in (0, 1]");
+  }
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(availableMarkerCount) || availableMarkerCount < 0) {
+    throw new Error("large-trade-detection: availableMarkerCount must be a non-negative safe integer");
+  }
+  const target = totalTradeCount === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalTradeCount * fraction));
+  const shown = Math.min(target, availableMarkerCount);
+  return Object.freeze({
+    target,
+    shown,
+    truncated: shown < target,
+    effectiveFraction: totalTradeCount === 0 ? 0 : shown / totalTradeCount,
+  });
+}

+/** Parses one REST aggTrades row: { a, p, q, f, l, T, m, M }. */
+export function parseAggTradeRow(raw: unknown): AggTradeRow | null {
+  if (!isRecord(raw)) return null;
+  const price = finiteNumber(raw.p);
+  const size = finiteNumber(raw.q);
+  if (price === undefined || size === undefined || price <= 0 || size <= 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.a) || (raw.a as number) < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.T) || (raw.T as number) < 0 || typeof raw.m !== "boolean") return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.f) || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.l) || (raw.f as number) < 0 || (raw.l as number) < (raw.f as number)) return null;
+  return { a: raw.a as number, p: price, q: size, f: raw.f as number, l: raw.l as number, T: raw.T as number, m: raw.m };
+}
+
+/**
+ * One REST page, validated as a whole: every row well formed, no more rows
+ * than asked, ids consecutive (Binance numbers aggregate trades without gaps
+ * per symbol) and, for a page asked by `fromId`, starting at it. A page that
+ * fails any of this is a failed read, never a shorter page: a dropped row
+ * would otherwise end the walk early and leave a hole inside claimed coverage.
+ */
+export function parseAggTradesPage(body: unknown, fromId: number | null): AggTradeRow[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(body)) throw new Error("aggTrades: body is not an array");
+  if (body.length > TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) throw new Error("aggTrades: more rows than asked");
+  const rows: AggTradeRow[] = [];
+  for (const raw of body) {
+    const row = parseAggTradeRow(raw);
+    if (row === null) throw new Error("aggTrades: malformed row");
+    rows.push(row);
+  }
+  rows.sort((a, b) => a.a - b.a);
+  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
+    if (rows[i].a !== rows[i - 1].a + 1) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not id-contiguous");
+    if (rows[i].T < rows[i - 1].T) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not in time order");
+  }
+  if (fromId !== null && rows.length > 0 && rows[0].a !== fromId) throw new Error("aggTrades: page does not start at fromId");
+  return rows;
+}
+
+/** Merges a closed stretch into the list, joining stretches that overlap or touch (adjacent milliseconds); the ends keep the ids of whichever stretch reaches furthest. */
+function addInterval(intervals: TapeInterval[], added: TapeInterval): void {
+  if (!(added.to >= added.from)) return;
+  let merged: TapeInterval = { ...added };
+  const apart: TapeInterval[] = [];
+  for (const interval of intervals) {
+    if (interval.to + 1 < merged.from || interval.from > merged.to + 1) {
+      apart.push(interval);
+      continue;
+    }
+    const start = interval.from < merged.from || (interval.from === merged.from && merged.startTradeId === undefined) ? interval : merged;
+    const end = interval.to > merged.to || (interval.to === merged.to && merged.endTradeId === undefined && merged.endAggId === undefined) ? interval : merged;
+    merged = { from: start.from, to: end.to, startTradeId: start.startTradeId, endTradeId: end.endTradeId, endAggId: end.endAggId };
+  }
+  apart.push(merged);
+  apart.sort((a, b) => a.from - b.from);
+  intervals.splice(0, intervals.length, ...apart);
+}
+
+/** The stretches with the given milliseconds cut out (an unverified boundary millisecond splits the stretch around it). */
+function subtractMilliseconds(intervals: TapeInterval[], milliseconds: readonly number[]): TapeInterval[] {
+  let result = intervals;
+  for (const ms of milliseconds) {
+    const next: TapeInterval[] = [];
+    for (const interval of result) {
+      if (ms < interval.from || ms > interval.to) {
+        next.push(interval);
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (ms > interval.from) next.push({ from: interval.from, to: ms - 1, startTradeId: interval.startTradeId });
+      if (ms < interval.to) next.push({ from: ms + 1, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId });
+    }
+    result = next;
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A bootstrap print as a trade frame. Aggregate trade ids are a separate
+ * sequence from the stream's trade ids, so they carry an `a` prefix: the two
+ * can never be mistaken for one another, and nothing is counted twice.
+ */
+function aggTradeFrame(selection: Selection, row: AggTradeRow): TradeFrame {
+  return { type: "trade", ...selection, ts_exchange: row.T, price: row.p, size: row.q, side: row.m ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${row.a}` };
+}
+
+function markerOf(trade: TradeFrame): MarkerFrame {
+  return { ...trade, type: "marker" };
+}
+
+function markerHistoryRow(marker: MarkerFrame): [number, number, number, "b" | "s", string] {
+  return [marker.ts_exchange, marker.price, marker.size, marker.side === "buy" ? "b" : "s", marker.trade_id];
+}
+
+function tradeOrder(a: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }, b: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }): number {
+  return a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange || (a.trade_id < b.trade_id ? -1 : a.trade_id > b.trade_id ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+/** The runtime's request validation (aggressive-history.js parseAggressiveHistoryRequest), minus the exact-key check the worker already applies. */
+export function parseAggressiveHistoryRequest(msg: Record<string, unknown>, nowMs: number): AggressiveHistoryRequest | null {
+  const { request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromMs, to_ts: toMs, min_size: minSize, cursor } = msg;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(requestId) || (requestId as number) < 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(fromMs) || (fromMs as number) < 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(toMs) || (toMs as number) <= (fromMs as number)) return null;
+  if ((toMs as number) > nowMs + AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_FUTURE_SKEW_MS) return null;
+  if ((toMs as number) - (fromMs as number) > MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS) return null;
+  if (typeof minSize !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(minSize) || minSize < 0) return null;
+  if (cursor !== undefined && (typeof cursor !== "string" || cursor.length === 0 || cursor.length > MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(cursor))) return null;
+  return { requestId: requestId as number, fromMs: fromMs as number, toMs: toMs as number, minSize, cursor: typeof cursor === "string" ? cursor : null };
+}
+
+function base64UrlEncode(text: string): string | null {
+  try {
+    return btoa(text).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+function base64UrlDecode(token: string): string | null {
+  try {
+    const padded = token.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/") + "=".repeat((4 - (token.length % 4)) % 4);
+    return atob(padded);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+/** A page cursor bound to its request (as the runtime's: version, instrument, window, floor, position), so a stale or foreign one is refused. */
+export function encodeHistoryCursor(selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest, last: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }): string | null {
+  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
+  const token = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify({
+    v: HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION, s: selection.symbol, k: scope, f: request.fromMs, t: request.toMs, m: request.minSize, x: last.ts_exchange, i: last.trade_id,
+  }));
+  return token !== null && token.length <= MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH ? token : null;
+}
+
+export function decodeHistoryCursor(token: string, selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest): { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string } | null {
+  const text = base64UrlDecode(token);
+  if (text === null) return null;
+  let decoded: unknown;
+  try {
+    decoded = JSON.parse(text);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (!isRecord(decoded)) return null;
+  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
+  if (
+    decoded.v !== HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION || decoded.s !== selection.symbol || decoded.k !== scope ||
+    decoded.f !== request.fromMs || decoded.t !== request.toMs || decoded.m !== request.minSize ||

+  ) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return { ts_exchange: decoded.x as number, trade_id: decoded.i };

+  /** Keeps the pair's tape state for the next instance or the next selection of the pair; the oldest pair goes when too many are kept. */
+  private keepTapeState(session: Session): void {
+    if (session.selection.provider !== "BINANCE") return;

+    tapeStates.delete(key);
+    tapeStates.set(key, {
+      markers: session.markers,
+      pool: session.pool,
+      observed: session.observed,
+      liveSpans: session.liveSpans,
+      unfillable: session.unfillable,
+      unverified: session.unverified,

+      recentTrades: session.recentTrades,
+      done: session.tape.done,
+    });
+    while (tapeStates.size > MAX_TAPE_STATES) {
+      const oldest = tapeStates.keys().next().value;
+      if (oldest === undefined) break;
+      tapeStates.delete(oldest);
+    }
+  }

+  /**
+   * The upstream stream was lost after delivering trades: the tick store on
+   * the main thread has a live span open that must end where the trades
+   * stopped. A lost runtime socket does exactly that through the worker's
+   * close and reconnect, so this transport closes the same way; the pair's
+   * tape state is kept for the instance the worker creates next, which
+   * reconciles the hole from REST or leaves it outside every claim.
+   */
+  private lostUpstream(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: the stream was lost after delivering trades; closing so the worker reconnects");
+    this.close();
+    const event = typeof CloseEvent === "function"
+      ? new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" })
+      : ({ code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" } as unknown as CloseEvent);
+    this.onclose?.(event);
   }

+
+  // ---- Tape-derived frames: markers, the aggressive pool, their history ----
+
+  /** Offers a print to the runtime's large-trade retention; an admitted one is broadcast as a `marker` when asked. */
+  private offerMarker(session: Session, trade: TradeFrame, broadcast: boolean): void {
+    const marker = markerOf(trade);
+    if (session.markers.insertAll([marker]) && broadcast) this.deliver(marker);
+  }
+
+  /** The newest markers the runtime would still hold: within its seven-day window, oldest first, the newest 5,000 for a snapshot. */
+  private retainedMarkers(session: Session): MarkerFrame[] {
+    const oldestKept = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
+    return session.markers.snapshot().filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange >= oldestKept);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Forgets coverage older than the pool's retention (nothing older can be
+   * claimed: the pool has no print for it) and bounds every list, so
+   * reconnects over a long session cannot grow them without end. The gap a
+   * seam fill is working on, and the stretches beside it, are kept.
+   */
+  private pruneCoverage(session: Session): void {
+    const cutoff = this.now() - POOL_WINDOW_MS;
+    const active = session.seam.gap;
+    const keepsActive = (interval: TapeInterval): boolean =>
+      active !== null && (interval.to === active.from || interval.from === active.to || (interval.from === active.from && interval.to === active.to));
+    const clip = (interval: TapeInterval): TapeInterval =>
+      interval.from >= cutoff ? interval : { from: cutoff, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId };
+    session.observed = session.observed.filter((interval) => interval.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(interval)).map(clip);
+    session.liveSpans = session.liveSpans.filter((span) => span.to >= cutoff);
+    session.unfillable = session.unfillable.filter((gap) => gap.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(gap));
+    session.unverified = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms >= cutoff);
+    if (session.observed.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.observed.splice(0, session.observed.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.liveSpans.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.liveSpans.splice(0, session.liveSpans.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.unfillable.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.unfillable.splice(0, session.unfillable.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.unverified.length > MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS) session.unverified.splice(0, session.unverified.length - MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Every stretch of tape observed in full: the closed ones, plus the open
+   * stream span reaching into the future while a stream delivers, merged
+   * where they touch, with every unverified boundary millisecond cut out.
+   */
+  private coverageIntervals(session: Session): TapeInterval[] {
+    this.pruneCoverage(session);
+    const intervals = session.observed.map((interval) => ({ ...interval }));
+    if (session.live !== null) addInterval(intervals, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, startTradeId: session.live.firstId });
+    return subtractMilliseconds(intervals, session.unverified);
+  }
+
+  /** Where the stretch of complete tape that reaches now begins, or null while no stream delivers. */
+  private coverageNowFrom(session: Session): number | null {
+    const open = this.coverageIntervals(session).find((interval) => interval.to === Infinity);
+    return open === undefined ? null : open.from;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * What the snapshot may claim: the pool's prints, its floor, and coverage
+   * from the start of the continuous observed stretch that reaches now (never
+   * before the pool's own window), or from now itself, an empty claim, while
+   * no stream delivers or a hole separates the stream from what was read.
+   */
+  private poolCoverage(session: Session): { prints: readonly TradeFrame[]; floor: number; from: number } {
+    const snapshot = session.pool.snapshot();
+    const nowMs = this.now();
+    const from = this.coverageNowFrom(session);
+    return { prints: snapshot.prints, floor: snapshot.floor, from: from === null ? nowMs : Math.min(nowMs, Math.max(snapshot.from, from)) };
+  }
+
+  /** The runtime's snapshot for the pair as this transport knows it: every closed bar, the last 100 live trades, the newest markers, the print pool. */
+  private snapshotFrame(session: Session): Record<string, unknown> {
+    const bars = [...session.closedBars.values()].sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
+    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
+    const pool = this.poolCoverage(session);
+    return {
+      type: "snapshot",
+      ...identityFields(session.selection),
+      bars: bars.map((row) => barFrame(session.selection, row, true, row.reconstructed)),
+      trades: [...session.recentTrades],
+      markers: markers.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS),
+      vpin_status: "DISABLED",
+      ...(session.tape.done || session.observed.length > 0 || session.live !== null
+        ? { aggressive_prints: [...pool.prints], aggressive_prints_floor: pool.floor, aggressive_prints_from: pool.from }
+        : {}),
+    };
+  }
+
+  /** The full marker set as marker_history frames, 5,000 rows each, `reset` on the first, as the runtime's markerHistoryFrames. */
+  private deliverMarkerHistory(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || !this.markerHistoryOptIn) return;
+    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
+    if (markers.length === 0) return;
+    session.markerHistorySeq = 0;
+    for (let start = 0; start < markers.length; start += MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS) {
+      this.deliver({
+        type: "marker_history",
+        ...identityFields(session.selection),
+        reset: start === 0,
+        seq: session.markerHistorySeq,
+        rows: markers.slice(start, start + MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS).map(markerHistoryRow),
+      });
+      session.markerHistorySeq += 1;
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** After the bootstrap or a seam fill: the snapshot again (as the runtime re-broadcasts it after a warm-up chunk), the forming bar, and the marker history for a client that opted in. */
+  private publishTapeState(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    session.seam.publishWhenSettled = false;
+    this.deliver(this.snapshotFrame(session));
+    const forming = session.lastFormingBar;
+    if (!session.recovering && forming !== null && forming.openTime > session.lastClosedOpen) this.deliver(barFrame(session.selection, forming, false, false));
+    this.deliverMarkerHistory(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The runtime answers from storage; this answers from the pool, and only
+   * claims completeness for a window that lies inside one observed stretch
+   * of tape (the bootstrap, a seam fill, a stream span), within the pool's
+   * window, at or above the pool's floor. Anything else, a hole between the
+   * REST pages and the stream or between two streams included, is honestly
+   * STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE, which the client retries three times and then rests.
+   * Pages of 10,000 with a cursor bound to the request, as the contract has it.
+   */
+  private aggressiveHistoryReply(session: Session, msg: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+    const request = parseAggressiveHistoryRequest(msg, this.now());
+    if (request === null) return null;
+    const after = request.cursor === null ? null : decodeHistoryCursor(request.cursor, session.selection, request);
+    if (request.cursor !== null && after === null) {
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: dropped aggressive_history request: cursor does not match the selected history window");
+      return null;
+    }
+    const reply = {
+      type: "aggressive_history",
+      ...identityFields(session.selection),
+      request_id: request.requestId,
+      from_ts: request.fromMs,
+      to_ts: request.toMs,
+      min_size: request.minSize,
+      retry_after_ms: null,
+    };
+    const pool = session.pool.snapshot();
+    const inside = this.coverageIntervals(session).some((interval) => interval.from <= request.fromMs && interval.to >= request.toMs - 1);
+    const covered = inside && request.fromMs >= pool.from && request.minSize >= pool.floor;
+    if (!covered) return { ...reply, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", next_cursor: null, trades: [] };
+    const matching = pool.prints
+      .filter((print) =>
+        print.ts_exchange >= request.fromMs && print.ts_exchange < request.toMs && print.size >= request.minSize &&
+        (after === null || tradeOrder(print, after) > 0))
+      .sort(tradeOrder);
+    const capped = matching.length > MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES;
+    const page = capped ? matching.slice(0, MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES) : matching;
+    const nextCursor = capped ? encodeHistoryCursor(session.selection, request, page[page.length - 1]) : null;
+    if (capped && nextCursor === null) return { ...reply, complete: false, reason: "CONVERSION_INCOMPLETE", next_cursor: null, trades: page };
+    return { ...reply, complete: !capped, reason: capped ? "CAP_REACHED" : null, next_cursor: nextCursor, trades: page };
+  }
+
+  private aggTradesUrl(session: Session, query: { fromId?: number; startTime?: number }): string {
+    const path = session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL" ? "/fapi/v1/aggTrades" : "/api/v3/aggTrades";
+    const params = new URLSearchParams({ symbol: session.binanceSymbol, limit: String(TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) });
+    if (query.fromId !== undefined) params.set("fromId", String(query.fromId));
+    if (query.startTime !== undefined) params.set("startTime", String(query.startTime));
+    return `${session.restBase}${path}?${params.toString()}`;
+  }
+
+  /** One validated page of aggregate trades, oldest first: the newest page, the page from `fromId`, or the page from `startTime` on. */
+  private async fetchAggTrades(session: Session, query: { fromId?: number; startTime?: number }, owner: { abort: AbortController | null }): Promise<AggTradeRow[]> {
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    owner.abort = controller;
+    const timer = this.setTimer(() => controller.abort(), REST_TIMEOUT_MS);
+    try {
+      const response = await this.fetchImpl(this.aggTradesUrl(session, query), { signal: controller.signal });
+      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`aggTrades: HTTP ${response.status}`);
+      return parseAggTradesPage(await response.json(), query.fromId ?? null);
+    } finally {
+      this.clearTimer(timer);
+      if (owner.abort === controller) owner.abort = null;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private startTapeBootstrap(session: Session): void {
+    if (session.tape.started) return;
+    session.tape.started = true;
+    void this.bootstrapTape(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The runtime seeds markers and the pool from storage; the browser reads
+   * recent aggregate trades from the mirror, newest page first by id, within a
+   * bounded page budget. The pages are staged, not seeded, until the stream's
+   * first trade is known (or no stream can deliver one that overlaps them):
+   * every row is then reconciled against the live tape by trade id before it
+   * is fed through the same retention and pool. The pages read are one
+   * id-contiguous stretch, recorded as observed; a page that fails validation
+   * or transport after others succeeded ends the walk with what was read; a
+   * first page that fails is retried with backoff, and after three such
+   * failures the READY health frame says the tape history is missing.
+   */
+  private async bootstrapTape(session: Session): Promise<void> {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    tape.inFlight = true;
+    const oldestWanted = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
+    let fromId: number | null = null;
+    let pages = 0;
+    try {
+      while (pages < MAX_TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGES) {
+        let rows: AggTradeRow[];
+        try {
+          rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? {} : { fromId }, tape);
+        } catch (error) {
+          if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+          if (pages === 0) throw error;
+          console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a trade history page failed; keeping what was read", error);
+          break;
+        }
+        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+        pages += 1;
+        if (rows.length === 0) break;
+        tape.staged.push(rows);
+        const oldest = rows[0];
+        if (rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE || oldest.a === 0 || oldest.T < oldestWanted) break;
+        fromId = Math.max(0, oldest.a - TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE);
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+      tape.inFlight = false;
+      tape.attempts += 1;
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: trade history request failed", error);
+      if (tape.attempts >= TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED && !tape.unavailable) {
+        tape.unavailable = true;
+        if (!session.degraded && !session.recovering) this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", this.readyReason(session));
+      }
+      this.scheduleTapeRetry(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    tape.inFlight = false;
+    tape.done = true;
+    const wasUnavailable = tape.unavailable;
+    tape.unavailable = false;
+    if (wasUnavailable && !session.degraded && !session.recovering) this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", HEALTH_REASON_READY);
+    this.commitStagedWhenSafe(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The staged pages can be reconciled against the live tape only once its
+   * first trade is known. With a stream delivering, or none that could ever
+   * deliver a trade older than the pages (no stream connecting or open), they
+   * commit at once; with a stream connecting or open but silent, a short
+   * grace keeps a first trade that is still in flight from overlapping
+   * already committed rows.
+   */
+  private commitStagedWhenSafe(session: Session): void {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    if (!tape.done || tape.staged.length === 0) return;
+    const streamPending = session.stream !== null && session.stream.readyState <= OPEN;
+    if (session.live === null && session.liveSpans.length === 0 && streamPending) {
+      if (tape.graceTimer !== null) return;
+      tape.graceTimer = this.setTimer(() => {
+        tape.graceTimer = null;
+        if (this.isCurrent(session)) this.commitStaged(session);
+      }, BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (tape.graceTimer !== null) {
+      this.clearTimer(tape.graceTimer);
+      tape.graceTimer = null;
+    }
+    this.commitStaged(session);
+  }
+
+  private commitStaged(session: Session): void {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    const staged = tape.staged;
+    tape.staged = [];
+    let kept = 0;
+    let from = Infinity;
+    let to = -Infinity;
+    let endAggId: number | undefined;
+    for (const page of staged) {
+      kept += this.seedTape(session, page);
+      for (const row of page) {
+        if (row.T < from) from = row.T;
+        if (row.T > to) to = row.T;
+        if (endAggId === undefined || row.a > endAggId) endAggId = row.a;
+      }
+    }
+    if (from <= to) addInterval(session.observed, { from, to, endAggId });
+    if (kept > 0 || from <= to) {
+      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
+      this.fillNextSeam(session);
+    }
+  }
+
+  private scheduleTapeRetry(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    if (tape.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(tape.timer);
+    const delay = tape.backoffMs;
+    tape.backoffMs = Math.min(tape.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
+    tape.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
+      tape.timer = null;
+      if (this.isCurrent(session)) void this.bootstrapTape(session);
+    }, delay);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Whether a REST row is tape the stream already delivered. Inside a live
+   * span (open or closed) it is; at a span's first or last millisecond the
+   * aggregate's fill ids decide: wholly before the first live trade or wholly
+   * after the last one it is missing tape and is kept, wholly within it is
+   * dropped, and an aggregate straddling the boundary cannot be told apart,
+   * so it is dropped and that millisecond is marked unverified: no coverage
+   * claim includes it.
+   */
+  private admitRestRow(session: Session, row: AggTradeRow): boolean {
+    const spans: readonly LiveSpan[] = session.live === null
+      ? session.liveSpans
+      : [...session.liveSpans, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, firstId: session.live.firstId, lastId: Infinity }];
+    for (const span of spans) {
+      if (row.T > span.from && row.T < span.to) return false;
+      if (row.T === span.from) {
+        if (row.l < span.firstId) continue;
+        if (row.f >= span.firstId) return false;
+        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
+        return false;
+      }
+      if (row.T === span.to) {
+        if (row.f > span.lastId) continue;
+        if (row.l <= span.lastId) return false;
+        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  private markUnverified(session: Session, ms: number): void {
+    if (!session.unverified.includes(ms)) session.unverified.push(ms);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * One validated page into the retention and the pool, as the runtime's
+   * warm-up seeds a storage chunk: rows from the calibration window (the
+   * newest fifteen minutes at the bootstrap's start) calibrate the floor
+   * (`calibrate: true`, every size counted), older rows are admitted at the
+   * floor; rows the stream already delivered are left out. Returns the rows kept.
+   */
+  private seedTape(session: Session, rows: readonly AggTradeRow[]): number {
+    const calibration: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    const older: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    let oldestTs = Infinity;
+    for (const row of rows) {
+      if (!this.admitRestRow(session, row)) continue;
+      const trade = aggTradeFrame(session.selection, row);
+      if (row.T < oldestTs) oldestTs = row.T;
+      (row.T >= session.calibrationFrom ? calibration : older).push(trade);
+      this.offerMarker(session, trade, false);
+    }
+    if (oldestTs === Infinity) return 0;
+    if (calibration.length > 0) session.pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs: oldestTs, calibrate: true });
+    if (older.length > 0) session.pool.seed(older, { fromMs: oldestTs });
+    return calibration.length + older.length;
+  }
+
+  /** The newest hole between two observed stretches (the open stream span counts) that is neither given up on nor made only of unverified milliseconds. */
+  private nextSeamGap(session: Session): TapeInterval | null {
+    const intervals = this.coverageIntervals(session);
+    for (let i = intervals.length - 1; i >= 1; i -= 1) {
+      const gap: TapeInterval = { from: intervals[i - 1].to, to: intervals[i].from, endAggId: intervals[i - 1].endAggId, endTradeId: intervals[i - 1].endTradeId };
+      if (session.unfillable.some((open) => open.from === gap.from && open.to === gap.to)) continue;
+      const interior = gap.to - gap.from - 1;
+      const unverifiedInside = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms > gap.from && ms < gap.to).length;
+      if (interior <= unverifiedInside) continue;
+      return gap;
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Closes the holes the tape has: between the bootstrap's newest row and the
+   * stream's first trade, or between one stream's last trade and the next
+   * stream's first. Kline recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the prints of
+   * such a hole are read from REST within a bounded budget: by id from the
+   * row after the last REST row when the hole follows one, else by time from
+   * the hole's first millisecond, every row reconciled against the live tape
+   * by fill id. A hole that cannot be closed stays a hole and is never inside
+   * a completeness claim. One fill runs at a time; when nothing is left to
+   * fill and a snapshot is waiting, it goes out.
+   */
+  private fillNextSeam(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || session.seam.inFlight || session.seam.timer !== null) return;
+    const gap = this.nextSeamGap(session);
+    if (gap === null) {
+      if (session.seam.publishWhenSettled) this.publishTapeState(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    void this.fillSeam(session, gap);
+  }
+
+  private async fillSeam(session: Session, gap: TapeInterval): Promise<void> {
+    const seam = session.seam;
+    seam.inFlight = true;
+    seam.gap = gap;
+    let closed = false;
+    let endAggId = gap.endAggId;
+    try {
+      let fromId: number | null = gap.endAggId === undefined ? null : gap.endAggId + 1;
+      for (let pages = 0; pages < MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES; pages += 1) {
+        const rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? { startTime: gap.from } : { fromId }, seam);
+        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+        const inside = rows.filter((row) => row.T <= gap.to);
+        if (inside.length > 0) {
+          this.seedTape(session, inside);
+          endAggId = inside[inside.length - 1].a;
+        }
+        // The hole is closed when a row past its end was read, or the page
+        // was short (nothing newer existed on the exchange at the read).
+        if (inside.length < rows.length || rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) {
+          closed = true;
+          break;
+        }
+        fromId = rows[rows.length - 1].a + 1;
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+      seam.inFlight = false;
+      seam.gap = null;
+      seam.failures += 1;
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam fill failed", error);
+      if (seam.failures < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP) {
+        const delay = seam.backoffMs;
+        seam.backoffMs = Math.min(seam.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
+        seam.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
+          seam.timer = null;
+          this.fillNextSeam(session);
+        }, delay);
+        return;
+      }
+      seam.failures = 0;
+      seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
+      this.fillNextSeam(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    seam.inFlight = false;
+    seam.gap = null;
+    seam.failures = 0;
+    seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+    if (closed) {
+      addInterval(session.observed, { from: gap.from, to: gap.to, endAggId });
+      // Coverage grew: the snapshot says so.
+      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
+    } else {
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam is wider than a fill may walk; it stays open");
+      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
+    }
+    this.fillNextSeam(session);
+  }
 }

+// The tape-derived frames of browser-direct mode: markers from the live tape
+// and from the REST bootstrap of recent aggregate trades, the aggressive-print
+// pool on the snapshot, aggressive_history answered from the pool, marker_history
+// for a client that opted in, and the honest states when the bootstrap fails.
+// The bootstrap pages come from the same 10,000 recorded BTC-USDT aggregate
+// trades the parity test drives the runtime modules with, so the markers on
+// the wire are compared with the runtime's own history buffer here as well.
+import { createRequire } from "node:module";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import {
+  BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS,
+  BrowserBinanceTransport,
+  DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS,
+  HEALTH_REASON_NO_TAPE_HISTORY,
+  HEALTH_REASON_READY,
+  MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES,
+  MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES,
+  MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS,
+  SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP,
+  STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS,
+  TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED,
+  TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE,
+  clearBrowserTapeStates,
+  parseAggTradesPage,
+  type UpstreamSocket,
+} from "../browserBinanceTransport";
+import { DEFAULT_WINDOW_MS as POOL_WINDOW_MS } from "../../lib/tape/aggressivePool";
+import { parseServerFrame, type MarkerFrame, type ServerFrame, type TradeFrame } from "../../types/frames";
+import { MARKER_BUCKET_MS, MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE } from "../../lib/tape/largeTradeDetection";
+import { loadRecordedTrades } from "./recordedTape";
+
+const requireRuntime = createRequire(import.meta.url);
+const { createHistoryBuffer } = requireRuntime(path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../../../runtime/src/server/history.js")) as {
+  createHistoryBuffer(options: { markerBucketMs: number; maxMarkersPerBucketSide: number }): {
+    recordMarker(marker: MarkerFrame): boolean;
+    snapshotMarkers(): MarkerFrame[];
+  };
+};
+
+const recorded = loadRecordedTrades();
+const FIRST_ID = Number(recorded[0].trade_id.slice(1));
+const LAST_ID = Number(recorded[recorded.length - 1].trade_id.slice(1));
+const OLDEST_TS = recorded[0].ts_exchange;
+const NEWEST_TS = recorded[recorded.length - 1].ts_exchange;
+// "now" is a few seconds after the newest recorded trade, inside the forming minute.
+const NOW_MS = NEWEST_TS + 5_000;
+const MINUTE = Math.floor(NOW_MS / 60_000) * 60_000;
+const SPOT = { type: "select", symbol: "BTC-USDT", provider: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT" };
+
+/** The mirror's aggTrades row for a recorded trade. */
+function aggTradeRow(trade: TradeFrame): Record<string, unknown> {
+  const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
+  return { a: id, p: String(trade.price), q: String(trade.size), f: id, l: id, T: trade.ts_exchange, m: trade.side === "sell", M: true };
+}
+
+/** Serves a tape as the mirror would: the newest page without parameters, the 1,000 ids from `fromId`, or the first 1,000 rows at or after `startTime`. */
+function recordedPage(url: string, rows: readonly TradeFrame[] = recorded): Record<string, unknown>[] {
+  const params = new URL(url).searchParams;
+  const fromId = params.get("fromId");
+  const startTime = params.get("startTime");
+  if (startTime !== null) return rows.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange >= Number(startTime)).slice(0, TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE).map(aggTradeRow);
+  const first = Number(rows[0].trade_id.slice(1));
+  const last = Number(rows[rows.length - 1].trade_id.slice(1));
+  const start = fromId === null ? Math.max(first, last - TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE + 1) : Number(fromId);
+  return rows.filter((trade) => {
+    const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
+    return id >= start && id < start + TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE;
+  }).map(aggTradeRow);
+}
+
+function historyRequest(requestId: number, fromTs: number, toTs: number, minSize: number, cursor?: string) {
+  return { type: "aggressive_history", request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromTs, to_ts: toTs, min_size: minSize, ...(cursor === undefined ? {} : { cursor }) };
+}
+
+/** Sends one aggressive_history request and returns its reply (or null when the transport drops it). */
+async function askHistory(h: Harness, request: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }> | null> {
+  const before = h.frames.length;
+  h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(request));
+  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+  const reply = h.parsed().slice(before).find((frame) => frame.type === "aggressive_history");
+  return reply === undefined ? null : reply as Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }>;
+}
+
+const klineRow = (openTime: number, close: string) => [openTime, "79500.00", "79600.00", "79400.00", close, "5.000", openTime + 59_999, "0", 50, "2.000", "0", "0"];
+const klines = [klineRow(MINUTE - 120_000, "79510.00"), klineRow(MINUTE - 60_000, "79520.00"), klineRow(MINUTE, "79530.00")];
+
+class FakeUpstreamSocket implements UpstreamSocket {
+  static instances: FakeUpstreamSocket[] = [];
+  readyState = 0;
+  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
+  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
+  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
+  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
+  readonly url: string;
+  constructor(url: string) {
+    this.url = url;
+    FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.push(this);
+  }
+  close(): void {
+    this.readyState = 3;
+  }
+  open(): void {
+    this.readyState = 1;
+    this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
+  }
+  message(payload: unknown): void {
+    this.onmessage?.(new MessageEvent("message", { data: JSON.stringify(payload) }));
+  }
+  drop(): void {
+    this.readyState = 3;
+    this.onclose?.(new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006 }));
+  }
+}
+
+type Harness = {
+  transport: BrowserBinanceTransport;
+  frames: Record<string, unknown>[];
+  parsed: () => ServerFrame[];
+  aggTradesMock: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+  stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket;
+  now: () => number;
+};
+
+/** The instance the worker creates after a transport closed: the same mirror, the same clock, the pair's kept tape state. */
+function reconnected(previous: Harness): Harness {
+  const previousAggTrades = previous.aggTradesMock as unknown as (url: string) => Promise<Response>;
+  const next = harness((url) => previousAggTrades(url), previous.now);
+  next.aggTradesMock = previous.aggTradesMock;
+  return next;
+}
+
+/** A hand-built mirror row with explicit fill ids. */
+function aggRow(a: number, T: number, f: number, l: number, q = "5.00000"): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return { a, p: "79530.00", q, f, l, T, m: false, M: true };
+}
+
+function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
+}
+
+function harness(aggTrades: (url: string) => Promise<Response> = async (url) => jsonResponse(recordedPage(url)), now: () => number = () => NOW_MS): Harness {
+  FakeUpstreamSocket.instances = [];
+  const aggTradesMock = vi.fn(aggTrades);
+  const transport = new BrowserBinanceTransport("ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws", {
+    fetch: ((url: string) => (url.includes("/aggTrades") ? aggTradesMock(url) : Promise.resolve(jsonResponse(klines)))) as unknown as typeof fetch,
+    WebSocket: FakeUpstreamSocket,
+    now,
+  });
+  const frames: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
+  transport.onmessage = (event) => {
+    frames.push(JSON.parse(event.data as string) as Record<string, unknown>);
+  };
+  return {
+    transport,
+    frames,
+    parsed: () => frames.map((frame) => {
+      const parsed = parseServerFrame(frame, { nowMs: now() });
+      if (parsed === null) throw new Error(`frame rejected by parseServerFrame: ${JSON.stringify(frame).slice(0, 400)}`);
+      return parsed;
+    }),
+    aggTradesMock,
+    stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.at(-1)!,
+    now,
+  };
+}
+
+async function openAndSelect(h: Harness, { markerHistory = true } = {}): Promise<void> {
+  h.transport.onopen = () => {
+    if (markerHistory) h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "capabilities", marker_history: true }));
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(SPOT));
+  };
+  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+  expect(h.transport.readyState).toBe(1);
+}
+
+/** Lets the sequential REST pages of the bootstrap resolve (each page is a few promise hops). */
+async function settle(): Promise<void> {
+  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i += 1) await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+}
+
+/** Lets a bootstrap read its pages and, with a stream connecting or open but silent, wait out the grace before committing them. */
+async function settleBootstrap(): Promise<void> {
+  await settle();
+  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS);
+  await settle();
+}
+
+function liveTrade(id: number, ts: number, size: string, sell: boolean, price = "79530.00") {
+  return { stream: "btcusdt@trade", data: { e: "trade", E: ts + 5, s: "BTCUSDT", t: id, p: price, q: size, T: ts, m: sell, M: true } };
+}
+
+function runtimeMarkers(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): MarkerFrame[] {
+  const buffer = createHistoryBuffer({ markerBucketMs: MARKER_BUCKET_MS, maxMarkersPerBucketSide: MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE });
+  for (const trade of trades) buffer.recordMarker({ ...trade, type: "marker" });
+  return buffer.snapshotMarkers();
+}
