+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.useFakeTimers();
+  clearBrowserTapeStates();
+});
+afterEach(() => {
+  vi.useRealTimers();
+});
+
+describe("BrowserBinanceTransport tape bootstrap", () => {
+  it("pages recent aggregate trades from the mirror and re-broadcasts a snapshot whose markers and pool pass the parser and equal the runtime's", async () => {
+    const h = harness();
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    await settleBootstrap();
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar", "snapshot", "bar", "marker_history"]);
+
+    // Ten full pages, newest first by id, then the page before the recording is empty.
+    expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(11);
+    expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls[0]![0])).toBe(`${DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS.spotRestBase}/api/v3/aggTrades?symbol=BTCUSDT&limit=1000`);
+    expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls[1]![0])).toBe(`${DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS.spotRestBase}/api/v3/aggTrades?symbol=BTCUSDT&limit=1000&fromId=${LAST_ID - 1_999}`);
+    expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls[10]![0])).toContain(`fromId=${FIRST_ID - 1_000}`);
+
+    const first = frames[1];
+    if (first.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    expect(first.markers).toEqual([]);
+    expect(first.aggressive_prints).toBeUndefined();
+
+    const snapshot = frames[5];
+    if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    expect(snapshot.bars.map((bar) => bar.ts_open)).toEqual(first.bars.map((bar) => bar.ts_open));
+    expect(snapshot.trades).toEqual([]);
+    expect(snapshot.vpin_status).toBe("DISABLED");
+
+    // Six minutes of tape, four per minute and side.
+    const expected = runtimeMarkers(recorded);
+    expect(expected.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+    expect(expected.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS);
+    expect(snapshot.markers).toEqual(expected);
+    expect(snapshot.markers.every((marker) => marker.trade_id.startsWith("a"))).toBe(true);
+
+    // No stream delivers yet: the prints from the pages are there, but no
+    // stretch of complete tape reaches now, so the snapshot claims none.
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(NOW_MS);
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    const prints = snapshot.aggressive_prints!;
+    expect(prints.length).toBeGreaterThan(100);
+    expect(prints.length).toBeLessThan(recorded.length);
+    for (let i = 1; i < prints.length; i += 1) expect(prints[i].ts_exchange).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(prints[i - 1].ts_exchange);
+    // Complete from the floor: every recorded print at or above it is in the pool.
+    const floor = snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor!;
+    const above = recorded.filter((trade) => trade.size >= floor).map((trade) => trade.trade_id);
+    const poolIds = new Set(prints.map((print) => print.trade_id));
+    expect(above.every((id) => poolIds.has(id))).toBe(true);
+
+    expect(frames[6]).toMatchObject({ type: "bar", ts_open: MINUTE, closed: false });
+    const history = frames[7];
+    if (history.type !== "marker_history") throw new Error("expected marker_history");
+    expect(history.reset).toBe(true);
+    expect(history.seq).toBe(0);
+    expect(history.markers).toEqual(expected);
+  });
+
+  it("sends no marker_history when the client did not opt in, and the full set when it opts in later", async () => {
+    const h = harness();
+    await openAndSelect(h, { markerHistory: false });
+    await settleBootstrap();
+    expect(h.frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar", "snapshot", "bar"]);
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "capabilities", marker_history: true }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["marker_history"]);
+    expect(frames[0]).toMatchObject({ reset: true, seq: 0 });
+    expect((frames[0] as { markers: unknown[] }).markers).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(recorded));
+  });
+
+  it("drops bootstrap rows at or after the first live trade so nothing is counted twice", async () => {
+    let release: (() => void) | null = null;
+    const h = harness((url) => new Promise<Response>((resolve) => {
+      release = () => resolve(jsonResponse(recordedPage(url)));
+    }));
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    h.stream().open();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    // A live trade from the middle of the recording's last page arrives before REST answers.
+    const cutoff = recorded[recorded.length - 300].ts_exchange;
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(9_000_000_001, cutoff, "0.00100", false));
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    for (let page = 0; page < 11; page += 1) {
+      await settle();
+      release!();
+    }
+    await settle();
+    const snapshot = h.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
+    if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    const kept = recorded.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange < cutoff);
+    expect(snapshot.markers.filter((marker) => marker.trade_id.startsWith("a"))).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(kept).filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange < cutoff));
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints!.every((print) => print.ts_exchange < cutoff || !print.trade_id.startsWith("a"))).toBe(true);
+    expect(snapshot.trades).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it("keeps what was read when a later page fails, with coverage starting at the oldest row read", async () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const h = harness(async (url) => {
+      calls += 1;
+      if (calls === 3) throw new Error("network");
+      return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url));
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h);
+      await settleBootstrap();
+      expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
+      const frames = h.parsed();
+      expect(frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar", "snapshot", "bar", "marker_history"]);
+      const snapshot = frames[5];
+      if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      const read = recorded.slice(-2_000);
+      expect(snapshot.markers).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(read));
+      expect(frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "health")).toHaveLength(1);
+      // Coverage is the two pages read, not the page that failed.
+      const floor = snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor!;
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, read[0].ts_exchange, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true, reason: null });
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(2, recorded[7_000].ts_exchange, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", trades: [] });
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("says the tape history is missing after three failed attempts, keeps marking live trades, and recovers when a later attempt succeeds", async () => {
+    let failing = true;
+    const h = harness(async (url) => {
+      if (failing) throw new Error("network");
+      return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url));
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h);
+      await settle();
+      expect(h.parsed().map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar"]);
+      h.frames.length = 0;
+      let delay = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+      for (let attempt = 2; attempt <= TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED; attempt += 1) {
+        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(delay);
+        await settle();
+        delay *= 2;
+      }
+      expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED);
+      expect(h.parsed()).toEqual([{ type: "health", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", recoveryState: "READY", stale: false, reason: HEALTH_REASON_NO_TAPE_HISTORY }]);
+      h.frames.length = 0;
+
+      // Markers still come from the live tape.
+      h.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      h.frames.length = 0;
+      h.stream().message(liveTrade(9_000_000_001, NOW_MS, "0.50000", false));
+      expect(h.parsed().map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["trade", "marker"]);
+      h.frames.length = 0;
+
+      // The next retry succeeds: READY is plain again, the seam between the
+      // pages and the live trade is read (nothing newer was recorded), and the
+      // snapshot goes out claiming coverage from the oldest page.
+      failing = false;
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(delay);
+      await settle();
+      const frames = h.parsed();
+      expect(frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["health", "snapshot", "bar", "marker_history"]);
+      expect(frames[0]).toMatchObject({ recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_READY });
+      expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.at(-1)![0])).toContain(`fromId=${LAST_ID + 1}`);
+      const snapshot = frames[1];
+      if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      expect(snapshot.trades).toHaveLength(1);
+      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(OLDEST_TS);
+      expect(snapshot.markers.some((marker) => marker.trade_id === "9000000001")).toBe(true);
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+});
+
+describe("BrowserBinanceTransport live markers", () => {
+  it("emits a marker right behind a trade the runtime's retention admits, and none for one it would not keep", async () => {
+    const h = harness(async () => jsonResponse([]));
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    await settle();
+    h.stream().open();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    const minute = MINUTE;
+    const sizes = ["1.00000", "0.90000", "0.80000", "0.70000"];
+    sizes.forEach((size, index) => h.stream().message(liveTrade(100 + index, minute + index * 1_000, size, true)));
+    // The fifth sell of the minute is the smallest: no marker.
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(104, minute + 4_000, "0.10000", true));
+    // A buy opens its own side of the bucket.
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(105, minute + 5_000, "0.10000", false));
+    // A bigger sell displaces the smallest retained one.
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(106, minute + 6_000, "2.00000", true));
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual([
+      "trade", "marker", "trade", "marker", "trade", "marker", "trade", "marker",
+      "trade",
+      "trade", "marker",
+      "trade", "marker",
+    ]);
+    expect(frames[1]).toEqual({ type: "marker", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: minute, price: 79530, size: 1, side: "sell", trade_id: "100" });
+    expect(frames[12]).toMatchObject({ type: "marker", trade_id: "106", size: 2 });
+    expect(frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "marker").map((frame) => (frame as MarkerFrame).trade_id)).toEqual(["100", "101", "102", "103", "105", "106"]);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("BrowserBinanceTransport aggressive_history", () => {
+  async function bootstrapped(): Promise<{ h: Harness; floor: number; from: number; prints: TradeFrame[] }> {
+    const h = harness();
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    await settleBootstrap();
+    const snapshot = h.parsed()[5];
+    if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    // The observed stretch is the recording itself; no stream delivers.
+    return { h, floor: snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor!, from: OLDEST_TS, prints: snapshot.aggressive_prints! };
+  }
+
+  it("answers a window inside the observed tape at the pool's floor completely, in time order", async () => {
+    const { h, floor, from, prints } = await bootstrapped();
+    const toTs = OLDEST_TS + 120_000;
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 3, from_ts: from, to_ts: toTs, min_size: floor }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames).toHaveLength(1);
+    const reply = frames[0];
+    if (reply.type !== "aggressive_history") throw new Error("expected aggressive_history");
+    expect(reply).toMatchObject({ request_id: 3, from_ts: from, to_ts: toTs, min_size: floor, complete: true, reason: null, next_cursor: null, retry_after_ms: null });
+    const expected = prints.filter((print) => print.ts_exchange >= from && print.ts_exchange < toTs);
+    expect(expected.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(reply.trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id)).toEqual(expected.map((trade) => trade.trade_id));
+    // A higher floor narrows it; a window inside the coverage is covered too.
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 4, from_ts: from + 60_000, to_ts: toTs, min_size: floor * 4 }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const narrower = h.parsed()[0];
+    if (narrower.type !== "aggressive_history") throw new Error("expected aggressive_history");
+    expect(narrower.complete).toBe(true);
+    expect(narrower.trades.every((trade) => trade.size >= floor * 4 && trade.ts_exchange >= from + 60_000)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("is honestly STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE before the bootstrap, outside the observed tape, past its end, or below the floor", async () => {
+    const early = harness(() => new Promise<Response>(() => {}));
+    await openAndSelect(early);
+    early.frames.length = 0;
+    early.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 1, from_ts: OLDEST_TS, to_ts: NOW_MS, min_size: 0 }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    expect(early.parsed()[0]).toMatchObject({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 1, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", next_cursor: null, trades: [] });
+
+    const { h, floor, from } = await bootstrapped();
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 5, from_ts: from - 1, to_ts: NEWEST_TS + 1, min_size: floor }));
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 6, from_ts: from, to_ts: NEWEST_TS + 1, min_size: floor / 2 }));
+    // Past the newest row read: the tape from there to now was never observed.
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 9, from_ts: from, to_ts: NOW_MS, min_size: floor }));
+    // Malformed or stale requests get no reply at all, as the runtime drops them.
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 7, from_ts: from, to_ts: NOW_MS + 120_000, min_size: floor }));
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 8, from_ts: from, to_ts: NOW_MS, min_size: floor, cursor: "bm90LWEtY3Vyc29y" }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames.map((frame) => (frame as { request_id: number }).request_id)).toEqual([5, 6, 9]);
+    for (const frame of frames) expect(frame).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", trades: [] });
+  });
+
+  it("pages a window of more than 10,000 prints with CAP_REACHED and a cursor bound to the request", async () => {
+    // Twelve pages of equal-sized prints: the floor is that size, every print is kept.
+    const synthetic: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 12_000; i += 1) {
+      synthetic.push({ type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: NOW_MS - 3_600_000 + i * 250, price: 79_500 + (i % 7), size: 0.25, side: i % 3 === 0 ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${500_000 + i}` });
+    }
+    const h = harness(async (url) => jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, synthetic)));
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    await settleBootstrap();
+    const snapshot = h.parsed()[5];
+    if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints).toHaveLength(12_000);
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor).toBe(0.25);
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    const from = synthetic[0].ts_exchange;
+    const request = { type: "aggressive_history", request_id: 20, from_ts: from, to_ts: synthetic[synthetic.length - 1].ts_exchange + 1, min_size: 0.25 };
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(request));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const page1 = h.parsed()[0];
+    if (page1.type !== "aggressive_history") throw new Error("expected aggressive_history");
+    expect(page1).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "CAP_REACHED", retry_after_ms: null });
+    expect(page1.trades).toHaveLength(MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES);
+    expect(typeof page1.next_cursor).toBe("string");
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ ...request, request_id: 21, cursor: page1.next_cursor }));
+    // The same cursor on another window is refused without a reply.
+    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ ...request, request_id: 22, from_ts: from + 1, cursor: page1.next_cursor }));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames).toHaveLength(1);
+    const page2 = frames[0];
+    if (page2.type !== "aggressive_history") throw new Error("expected aggressive_history");
+    expect(page2).toMatchObject({ request_id: 21, complete: true, reason: null, next_cursor: null });
+    expect(page2.trades).toHaveLength(2_000);
+    const ids = [...page1.trades, ...page2.trades].map((trade) => trade.trade_id);
+    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(12_000);
+    expect(ids).toEqual(synthetic.map((trade) => trade.trade_id));
+  });
+});
+
+describe("BrowserBinanceTransport tape seams", () => {
+  const SEAM_SIZE = "5.00000";
+  /** Rows the mirror holds after the recording: two inside the seam, one at the live trade's time and one after it. */
+  function seamRows(liveFrom: number): TradeFrame[] {
+    return [NEWEST_TS + 1_000, NEWEST_TS + 2_000, liveFrom, liveFrom + 500].map((ts, index) => ({
+      type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: ts, price: 79_530, size: Number(SEAM_SIZE), side: "buy", trade_id: `a${LAST_ID + 1 + index}`,
+    }));
+  }
+
+  it("claims nothing across the seam when the stream connects after the bootstrap, then fills the seam from REST and claims the whole stretch", async () => {
+    const liveFrom = NEWEST_TS + 3_000;
+    const tape = [...recorded, ...seamRows(liveFrom)];
+    let release: (() => void) | null = null;
+    const h = harness((url) => {
+      if (!url.includes(`fromId=${LAST_ID + 1}`)) return Promise.resolve(jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, recorded)));
+      return new Promise<Response>((resolve) => {
+        release = () => resolve(jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, tape)));
+      });
+    });
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    await settleBootstrap();
+    const first = h.parsed()[5];
+    if (first.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    const floor = first.aggressive_prints_floor!;
+    expect(first.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(NOW_MS);
+    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, NOW_MS, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(2, OLDEST_TS, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+
+    // The stream connects late; its first trade opens the live span and starts the seam fill.
+    h.stream().open();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    // Its trade id is below the mirror's fill ids at that millisecond, so those rows are the stream's.
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(100, liveFrom, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+    // The pages ended at an aggregate id: the seam is read from the next id, no millisecond ambiguity.
+    expect(String(h.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.at(-1)![0])).toContain(`fromId=${LAST_ID + 1}`);
+    // Until the fill lands, the hole between the newest page and the first
+    // live trade is inside no claim (the live print's size has moved the
+    // floor, so these ask well above it).
+    const aboveFloor = 1;
+    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(3, OLDEST_TS, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(4, liveFrom, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+    expect(h.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(0);
+
+    release!();
+    await settle();
+    const frames = h.parsed();
+    expect(frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
+    const snapshot = frames.find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")!;
+    if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(OLDEST_TS);
+    // The seam's own prints are in the pool; the rows at and after the live trade were the stream's and are not.
+    const ids = snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
+    expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 1}`);
+    expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 2}`);
+    expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 3}`);
+    expect(ids).toContain("100");
+    const whole = await askHistory(h, historyRequest(5, OLDEST_TS, liveFrom + 1_000, aboveFloor));
+    expect(whole).toMatchObject({ complete: true, reason: null });
+    expect(whole!.trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id).slice(-3)).toEqual([`a${LAST_ID + 1}`, `a${LAST_ID + 2}`, "100"]);
+  });
+
+  it("closes itself when a stream that delivered trades is lost, as a lost runtime socket would, and the next instance keeps the tape and leaves the hole outside every claim until REST can fill it", async () => {
+    let seamFails = true;
+    const holeRows: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    const h1 = harness(async (url) => {
+      if (!url.includes("startTime")) return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url));
+      if (seamFails) throw new Error("network");
+      return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url, holeRows));
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h1);
+      await settleBootstrap();
+      h1.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      const t1 = NEWEST_TS + 1_000;
+      const t2 = NEWEST_TS + 1_500;
+      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(1, t1, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(2, t2, SEAM_SIZE, true));
+      await settle();
+      const bootstrapReads = h1.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.length;
+      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(1, t1, t2 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      h1.frames.length = 0;
+      const closed = vi.fn();
+      h1.transport.onclose = closed;
+      h1.stream().drop();
+      // Not RECOVERING with the pair still "live": the transport is gone, the worker reconnects.
+      expect(h1.transport.readyState).toBe(3);
+      expect(closed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+      expect(h1.frames).toEqual([]);
+
+      const h2 = reconnected(h1);
+      await openAndSelect(h2);
+      await settle();
+      // No second bootstrap: the kept markers and pool ride on the connect snapshot, which claims nothing (no stream delivers).
+      expect(h2.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.length).toBe(bootstrapReads);
+      const connectFrames = h2.parsed();
+      expect(connectFrames.map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar", "marker_history"]);
+      const connectSnapshot = connectFrames[1];
+      if (connectSnapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      expect(connectSnapshot.markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+      expect(connectSnapshot.trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id)).toEqual(["1", "2"]);
+      expect(connectSnapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(NOW_MS);
+      // The closed span is observed up to its last trade only.
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(2, t1, t2 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(3, t1, t2 + 2, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+
+      h2.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      h2.frames.length = 0;
+      const t3 = NEWEST_TS + 2_500;
+      h2.stream().message(liveTrade(3, t3, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      // The span before ended at a trade id: the seam is read from its last millisecond and reconciled by fill id.
+      expect(String(h2.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.at(-1)![0])).toContain(`startTime=${t2}`);
+      // Three failed seam reads with backoff: the hole stays open, every claim stops at it.
+      let delay = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+      for (let failure = 1; failure < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP; failure += 1) {
+        await settle();
+        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(delay);
+        delay *= 2;
+      }
+      await settle();
+      expect(h2.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.length).toBe(bootstrapReads + SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP);
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(4, t1, t3 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", trades: [] });
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(5, t3, t3 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(6, OLDEST_TS, t2 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(h2.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(0);
+      h2.stream().drop();
+      expect(h2.transport.readyState).toBe(3);
+
+      // The next outage's seam can be read: the mirror holds one print inside the hole and one past it.
+      holeRows.push({ type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: t3 + 700, price: 79_530, size: 5, side: "sell", trade_id: "a77" });
+      const t4 = NEWEST_TS + 4_000;
+      holeRows.push({ type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", ts_exchange: t4 + 100, price: 79_530, size: 5, side: "sell", trade_id: "a78" });
+      seamFails = false;
+      const h3 = reconnected(h2);
+      await openAndSelect(h3);
+      await settle();
+      h3.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      h3.frames.length = 0;
+      h3.stream().message(liveTrade(4, t4, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      await settle();
+      expect(String(h3.aggTradesMock.mock.calls.at(-1)![0])).toContain(`startTime=${t3}`);
+      // The first hole was given up and is not re-read; the second is closed and the snapshot says so.
+      const snapshot = h3.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
+      if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(t3);
+      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id)).toContain("a77");
+      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id)).not.toContain("a78");
+      expect(await askHistory(h3, historyRequest(7, t3, t4 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(await askHistory(h3, historyRequest(8, t1, t4 + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("reconciles trades sharing a millisecond at both seam boundaries by fill id, and keeps an unverifiable millisecond outside every claim", async () => {
+    const C = NEWEST_TS + 3_000;
+    const L = C + 1_000;
+    const M = C + 5_000;
+    const h1 = harness(async (url) => {
+      if (url.includes(`fromId=${LAST_ID + 1}`)) {
+        // The seam after the pages: one row before the live span; at its first
+        // millisecond one wholly before the first live trade (kept), one
+        // straddling it (unverifiable) and one wholly after (the stream's).
+        return jsonResponse([
+          aggRow(LAST_ID + 1, NEWEST_TS + 1_000, 400, 400),
+          aggRow(LAST_ID + 2, C, 490, 495),
+          aggRow(LAST_ID + 3, C, 498, 500),
+          aggRow(LAST_ID + 4, C, 505, 507),
+          aggRow(LAST_ID + 5, C + 10, 508, 508),
+        ]);
+      }
+      if (url.includes(`startTime=${L}`)) {
+        // The seam after a lost stream whose last trade was id 502 at L: one
+        // wholly delivered, one wholly after it (kept), one straddling it.
+        return jsonResponse([
+          aggRow(9_001, L, 501, 502),
+          aggRow(9_002, L, 503, 504),
+          aggRow(9_003, L, 502, 503),
+          aggRow(9_004, M + 5, 700, 700),
+        ]);
+      }
+      return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url));
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h1);
+      await settleBootstrap();
+      h1.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      h1.frames.length = 0;
+      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(500, C, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(501, C, SEAM_SIZE, true));
+      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(502, L, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      await settle();
+      const snapshot = h1.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
+      if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      const ids = snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
+      expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 1}`);
+      expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 2}`);
+      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 3}`);
+      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 4}`);
+      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 5}`);
+      expect(ids).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["500", "501", "502"]));
+      // Millisecond C holds a fill nobody can place: coverage reaching now starts after it.
+      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(C + 1);
+      expect(h1.transport.tapeCoverage.unverified).toBe(1);
+      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, C, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(2, OLDEST_TS, C + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(3, C + 1, L + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+
+      h1.stream().drop();
+      const h2 = reconnected(h1);
+      await openAndSelect(h2);
+      await settle();
+      h2.stream().open();
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      h2.frames.length = 0;
+      h2.stream().message(liveTrade(600, M, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+      await settle();
+      const after = h2.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
+      if (after === undefined || after.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      const afterIds = after.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
+      expect(afterIds).toContain("a9002");
+      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9001");
+      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9003");
+      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9004");
+      expect(h2.transport.tapeCoverage.unverified).toBe(2);
+      expect(after.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(L + 1);
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(4, C + 1, L, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(5, C + 1, L + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(6, L + 1, M + 1_000, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("commits the bootstrap pages only once the stream's first trade can be reconciled, so a page that landed first is not counted twice", async () => {
+    const h = harness();
+    await openAndSelect(h);
+    h.stream().open();
+    await settle();
+    // Every page is read while the stream is open but silent: staged, not committed.
+    expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(11);
+    expect(h.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(10);
+    expect(h.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, NEWEST_TS + 1, 0))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+    // The first live trade lands inside the newest page (the stream connected before REST answered).
+    const overlap = recorded[recorded.length - 300];
+    const C = overlap.ts_exchange;
+    const t0 = Number(overlap.trade_id.slice(1));
+    h.frames.length = 0;
+    h.stream().message(liveTrade(t0, C, "0.00100", false));
+    await settle();
+    const snapshot = h.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
+    if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+    const kept = recorded.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange < C || (trade.ts_exchange === C && Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1)) < t0));
+    expect(snapshot.markers.filter((marker) => marker.trade_id.startsWith("a"))).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(kept).filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange < C || Number(marker.trade_id.slice(1)) < t0));
+    const restIds = new Set(kept.map((trade) => trade.trade_id));
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints!.every((print) => !print.trade_id.startsWith("a") || restIds.has(print.trade_id))).toBe(true);
+    expect(h.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(0);
+    // The pages reach into the live span: one stretch, claimed from the oldest row.
+    expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(OLDEST_TS);
+  });
+
+  it("commits the staged pages after a short grace while the stream is connecting or open but silent", async () => {
+    const silent = harness();
+    await openAndSelect(silent);
+    await settle();
+    // Every page read while the socket was still connecting: staged.
+    expect(silent.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(10);
+    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
+    silent.stream().open();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS - 1);
+    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
+    await settle();
+    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(silent.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it("bounds the coverage bookkeeping across many reconnects and forgets what is past the pool's retention", async () => {
+    const clock = { now: NOW_MS };
+    const nowFn = () => clock.now;
+    let h = harness(async (url) => {
+      if (url.includes("startTime")) throw new Error("network");
+      return jsonResponse([]);
+    }, nowFn);
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      const rounds = 200;
+      const stepMs = 2 * 60_000;
+      for (let round = 0; round < rounds; round += 1) {
+        if (round > 0) h = reconnected(h);
+        await openAndSelect(h);
+        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+        h.stream().open();
+        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+        h.stream().message(liveTrade(round + 1, clock.now - 1_000, SEAM_SIZE, false));
+        // Let the seam fill fail its three reads, then move on.
+        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS * 4);
+        const coverage = h.transport.tapeCoverage;
+        expect(coverage.observed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+        expect(coverage.liveSpans).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+        expect(coverage.unfillable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+        clock.now += stepMs;
+        h.stream().drop();
+      }
+      // Six and a half hours of two-minute rounds: only the last four hours remain.
+      const h2 = reconnected(h);
+      await openAndSelect(h2);
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
+      const kept = h2.transport.tapeCoverage;
+      const withinRetention = Math.ceil(POOL_WINDOW_MS / stepMs) + 1;
+      expect(kept.liveSpans).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
+      expect(kept.liveSpans).toBeGreaterThan(withinRetention - 3);
+      expect(kept.observed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
+      expect(kept.unfillable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
+      expect(kept.observed).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+});
+
+describe("BrowserBinanceTransport page integrity", () => {
+  it("refuses a page with a malformed row, a gap in its ids, more rows than asked or a wrong start", () => {
+    const good = recorded.slice(0, 5).map(aggTradeRow);
+    expect(parseAggTradesPage(good, FIRST_ID)).toHaveLength(5);
+    expect(() => parseAggTradesPage([...good.slice(0, 2), { ...good[2], q: "x" }, ...good.slice(3)], null)).toThrow("malformed row");
+    expect(() => parseAggTradesPage([...good.slice(0, 2), ...good.slice(3)], null)).toThrow("id-contiguous");
+    expect(() => parseAggTradesPage(good, FIRST_ID + 1)).toThrow("fromId");
+    expect(() => parseAggTradesPage(new Array(TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE + 1).fill(good[0]), null)).toThrow("more rows");
+    expect(() => parseAggTradesPage("nope", null)).toThrow("not an array");
+  });
+
+  it("treats a full page with one malformed row as a failed read: retried, never a shorter page that ends the walk with a hole inside claimed coverage", async () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const h = harness(async (url) => {
+      calls += 1;
+      const page = recordedPage(url);
+      if (calls === 1) page[500] = { ...page[500], p: "not-a-price" };
+      return jsonResponse(page);
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h);
+      await settle();
+      // The first attempt failed on its first page: nothing published, nothing claimed.
+      expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+      expect(h.parsed().map((frame) => frame.type)).toEqual(["selection", "snapshot", "health", "book", "bar"]);
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, NEWEST_TS + 1, 0))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
+      // The retry reads the same page whole.
+      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS);
+      await settleBootstrap();
+      expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(12);
+      const snapshot = h.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot" && frame.markers.length > 0);
+      if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      expect(snapshot.markers).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(recorded));
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(2, OLDEST_TS, NEWEST_TS + 1, snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor!))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("ends the walk at a later malformed page and claims only the pages validated before it", async () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const h = harness(async (url) => {
+      calls += 1;
+      const page = recordedPage(url);
+      if (calls === 2) page.splice(10, 1);
+      return jsonResponse(page);
+    });
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    try {
+      await openAndSelect(h);
+      await settleBootstrap();
+      expect(h.aggTradesMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
+      const snapshot = h.parsed()[5];
+      if (snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
+      const read = recorded.slice(-1_000);
+      expect(snapshot.markers).toEqual(runtimeMarkers(read));
+      const floor = snapshot.aggressive_prints_floor!;
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(1, read[0].ts_exchange, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
+      // The page with the missing row is not coverage: a window reaching into it is unavailable.
+      expect(await askHistory(h, historyRequest(2, recorded[8_500].ts_exchange, NEWEST_TS + 1, floor))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", trades: [] });
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore();
+    }
+  });
+});

diff --git a/apps/web/src/workers/tests/recordedTape.ts b/apps/web/src/workers/tests/recordedTape.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..28657a0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/web/src/workers/tests/recordedTape.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+// The recorded tape the tape tests share: 10,000 BTC-USDT aggregate trades read
+// from Binance's public mirror (/api/v3/aggTrades, ten pages of 1,000) on
+// 2026-10-26, stored as [aggTradeId, price, quantity, time, buyerIsMaker] rows,
+// gzipped. Loaded as trade frames the way browser-direct mode frames them
+// (`a` prefix on the aggregate trade id).
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { gunzipSync } from "node:zlib";
+import type { TradeFrame } from "../../types/frames";
+
+type CompactRow = [a: number, p: string, q: string, T: number, m: 0 | 1];
+
+export function loadRecordedTrades(): TradeFrame[] {
+  const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+  const packed = readFileSync(path.join(here, "fixtures/binance-mirror/aggtrades-btcusdt-10k.json.gz"));
+  const rows = JSON.parse(gunzipSync(packed).toString("utf8")) as CompactRow[];
+  return rows.map(([a, p, q, T, m]) => ({
+    type: "trade", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT",
+    ts_exchange: T, price: Number(p), size: Number(q), side: m === 1 ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${a}`,
+  }));
+}

+// Parity between the browser's tape derivations (lib/tape, lib/chart/markerRetention)
+// and the runtime's CommonJS modules they mirror. vitest runs in Node, so the
+// runtime files are required as they are and driven with the same 10,000
+// recorded BTC-USDT aggregate trades (fixtures/binance-mirror/aggtrades-btcusdt-10k.json.gz,
+// read from Binance's public mirror on 2026-10-26). Any divergence is a bug on
+// one side; the runtime is the reference.
+import { createRequire } from "node:module";
+import path from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { MarkerFrame, TradeFrame } from "../../types/frames";
+import { createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer } from "../../lib/chart/markerRetention";
+import * as detection from "../../lib/tape/largeTradeDetection";
+import { createAggressivePool, createPrintFloor, floorAbove, type AggressivePool } from "../../lib/tape/aggressivePool";
+import { loadRecordedTrades } from "./recordedTape";
+
+const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const requireRuntime = createRequire(import.meta.url);
+const runtimeSrc = path.resolve(here, "../../../../runtime/src");
+
+type RuntimeDetection = {
+  usesContractMagnitude(provider: string, assetClass: string | undefined): boolean;
+  tradeMagnitude(provider: string, assetClass: string | undefined, price: number, size: number): number;
+  markerMagnitude(marker: MarkerFrame): number;
+  lowerMagnitudePriority(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean;
+  resolveTopFractionCount(total: number, fraction: number, available: number): detection.TopFractionCount;
+  [constant: string]: unknown;
+};
+type RuntimeHistoryBuffer = {
+  recordMarker(marker: MarkerFrame): boolean;
+  wouldRetainMarker(ts: number, side: string, magnitude: number): boolean;
+  snapshotMarkers(): MarkerFrame[];
+  markerCount(): number;
+};
+type RuntimePool = {
+  record(trade: TradeFrame): boolean;
+  seed(trades: readonly TradeFrame[], options?: { fromMs?: number; calibrate?: boolean }): number;
+  snapshot(): { prints: readonly TradeFrame[]; floor: number; from: number };
+  gate(): number;
+  readonly size: number;
+  readonly floor: number;
+};
+
+const runtimeDetection = requireRuntime(path.join(runtimeSrc, "indicators/large-trade/large-trade-detection.js")) as RuntimeDetection;
+const { createHistoryBuffer } = requireRuntime(path.join(runtimeSrc, "server/history.js")) as {
+  createHistoryBuffer(options: { markerBucketMs: number; maxMarkersPerBucketSide: number }): RuntimeHistoryBuffer;
+};
+const { createAggressivePool: createRuntimePool } = requireRuntime(path.join(runtimeSrc, "server/aggressive-pool.js")) as {
+  createAggressivePool(options: { windowMs?: number; maxPrints?: number; now: () => number }): RuntimePool;
+};
+const { createPrintFloor: createRuntimePrintFloor } = requireRuntime(path.join(runtimeSrc, "indicators/passive_liquidity/print-floor.js")) as {
+  createPrintFloor(options: { window: number; share: number; minSamples: number }): { push(size: number): void; value(): number };
+};
+const { floorAbove: runtimeFloorAbove } = requireRuntime(path.join(runtimeSrc, "indicators/passive_liquidity/bounded-prints.js")) as {
+  floorAbove(size: number): number;
+};
+
+const trades = loadRecordedTrades();
+const markers: MarkerFrame[] = trades.map((trade) => ({ ...trade, type: "marker" }));
+
+/** A deterministic PRNG so the pairwise checks are the same run after run. */
+function lcg(seed: number): () => number {
+  let state = seed >>> 0;
+  return () => {
+    state = (Math.imul(state, 1_664_525) + 1_013_904_223) >>> 0;
+    return state / 0x1_0000_0000;
+  };
+}
+
+describe("tape parity: the recorded tape", () => {
+  it("is 10,000 trades of one pair with unique ids, in time order", () => {
+    expect(trades).toHaveLength(10_000);
+    expect(new Set(trades.map((trade) => trade.trade_id)).size).toBe(10_000);
+    for (let i = 1; i < trades.length; i += 1) expect(trades[i].ts_exchange).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(trades[i - 1].ts_exchange);
+    expect(trades.every((trade) => trade.price > 0 && trade.size > 0)).toBe(true);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("tape parity: large-trade-detection.js", () => {
+  it("shares every constant", () => {
+    for (const name of ["CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_PROVIDER", "CONTRACT_MAGNITUDE_ASSET_CLASS", "MARKER_BUCKET_MS", "MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE", "MIN_TOP_FRACTION", "MAX_TOP_FRACTION", "DEFAULT_TOP_FRACTION"] as const) {
+      expect(detection[name]).toBe(runtimeDetection[name]);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("ranks every recorded print by the same magnitude", () => {
+    for (const marker of markers) {
+      expect(detection.markerMagnitude(marker)).toBe(runtimeDetection.markerMagnitude(marker));
+      expect(detection.tradeMagnitude("BINANCE", undefined, marker.price, marker.size)).toBe(runtimeDetection.tradeMagnitude("BINANCE", undefined, marker.price, marker.size));
+    }
+    expect(detection.tradeMagnitude("DXFEED", "FUTURE", 5_000, 3)).toBe(runtimeDetection.tradeMagnitude("DXFEED", "FUTURE", 5_000, 3));
+    expect(detection.tradeMagnitude("DXFEED", "EQUITY", 5_000, 3)).toBe(runtimeDetection.tradeMagnitude("DXFEED", "EQUITY", 5_000, 3));
+    expect(detection.usesContractMagnitude("DXFEED", "FUTURE")).toBe(runtimeDetection.usesContractMagnitude("DXFEED", "FUTURE"));
+  });
+
+  it("breaks every tie the same way over 10,000 random pairs", () => {
+    const random = lcg(7);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 10_000; i += 1) {
+      const a = markers[Math.floor(random() * markers.length)];
+      const b = markers[Math.floor(random() * markers.length)];
+      expect(detection.lowerMagnitudePriority(a, b)).toBe(runtimeDetection.lowerMagnitudePriority(a, b));
+    }
+    const twin = { ...markers[0], trade_id: "a0" };
+    expect(detection.lowerMagnitudePriority(markers[0], twin)).toBe(runtimeDetection.lowerMagnitudePriority(markers[0], twin));
+  });
+
+  it("resolves the same top-fraction counts", () => {
+    for (const total of [0, 1, 7, 321, 10_000, 1_234_567]) {
+      for (const fraction of [0.0002, 0.0005, 0.002, 0.0077, 0.02, 1]) {
+        for (const available of [0, 1, 4, 300, 5_000]) {
+          expect(detection.resolveTopFractionCount(total, fraction, available)).toEqual(runtimeDetection.resolveTopFractionCount(total, fraction, available));
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    expect(() => detection.resolveTopFractionCount(10, 0, 1)).toThrow();
+    expect(() => runtimeDetection.resolveTopFractionCount(10, 0, 1)).toThrow();
+  });
+});
+
+describe("tape parity: history.js marker retention", () => {
+  it("admits the same prints as markers, trade by trade, and holds the same set afterwards", () => {
+    const runtime = createHistoryBuffer({ markerBucketMs: detection.MARKER_BUCKET_MS, maxMarkersPerBucketSide: detection.MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE });
+    const browser = createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer(100_000, detection.MARKER_BUCKET_MS, detection.MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE);
+    let admitted = 0;
+    for (const marker of markers) {
+      // The server only builds a marker frame when the buffer would retain it.
+      const wouldRetain = runtime.wouldRetainMarker(marker.ts_exchange, marker.side, marker.price * marker.size);
+      const recorded = runtime.recordMarker(marker);
+      if (!wouldRetain) expect(recorded).toBe(false);
+      expect(browser.insertAll([marker])).toBe(recorded);
+      if (recorded) admitted += 1;
+    }
+    expect(admitted).toBeGreaterThan(markers.length / 100);
+    const expected = runtime.snapshotMarkers();
+    const actual = browser.snapshot();
+    expect(actual.map((marker) => marker.trade_id)).toEqual(expected.map((marker) => marker.trade_id));
+    expect(actual).toEqual(expected);
+    expect(browser.size()).toBe(runtime.markerCount());
+    expect(expected.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(expected.length).toBeLessThan(markers.length);
+    // Never more than four per minute and side.
+    const perBucket = new Map<string, number>();
+    for (const marker of expected) {
+      const key = `${Math.floor(marker.ts_exchange / detection.MARKER_BUCKET_MS)}:${marker.side}`;
+      perBucket.set(key, (perBucket.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
+    }
+    expect(Math.max(...perBucket.values())).toBeLessThanOrEqual(detection.MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE);
+  });
+
+  it("agrees when the tape arrives out of order and with duplicates", () => {
+    const random = lcg(11);
+    const shuffled = [...markers].sort(() => random() - 0.5);
+    const withDuplicates = [...shuffled, ...shuffled.slice(0, 500)];
+    const runtime = createHistoryBuffer({ markerBucketMs: detection.MARKER_BUCKET_MS, maxMarkersPerBucketSide: detection.MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE });
+    const browser = createTimeCoveredMarkerRetentionBuffer(100_000, detection.MARKER_BUCKET_MS, detection.MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE);
+    for (const marker of withDuplicates) expect(browser.insertAll([marker])).toBe(runtime.recordMarker(marker));
+    expect(browser.snapshot()).toEqual(runtime.snapshotMarkers());
+  });
+});
+
+describe("tape parity: print-floor.js and bounded-prints.js", () => {
+  it("computes the same floor after every size", () => {
+    const runtime = createRuntimePrintFloor({ window: 4_096, share: 0.02, minSamples: 200 });
+    const browser = createPrintFloor({ window: 4_096, share: 0.02, minSamples: 200 });
+    for (const trade of trades) {
+      runtime.push(trade.size);
+      browser.push(trade.size);
+      expect(browser.value()).toBe(runtime.value());
+    }
+    expect(browser.value()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    const small = createRuntimePrintFloor({ window: 16, share: 0.25, minSamples: 4 });
+    const smallBrowser = createPrintFloor({ window: 16, share: 0.25, minSamples: 4 });
+    for (const trade of trades.slice(0, 2_000)) {
+      small.push(trade.size);
+      smallBrowser.push(trade.size);
+      expect(smallBrowser.value()).toBe(small.value());
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("steps above a size identically", () => {
+    for (const size of [0, 1e-8, 0.5, 1, 12.345, 1e6, 1e9]) expect(floorAbove(size)).toBe(runtimeFloorAbove(size));
+  });
+});
+
+type PoolPair = { runtime: RuntimePool; browser: AggressivePool<TradeFrame> };
+
+function expectSamePool({ runtime, browser }: PoolPair): void {
+  expect(browser.floor).toBe(runtime.floor);
+  expect(browser.gate()).toBe(runtime.gate());
+  expect(browser.size).toBe(runtime.size);
+  const expected = runtime.snapshot();
+  const actual = browser.snapshot();
+  expect(actual.floor).toBe(expected.floor);
+  expect(actual.from).toBe(expected.from);
+  expect(actual.prints.map((print) => print.trade_id)).toEqual(expected.prints.map((print) => print.trade_id));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Drives both pools as the transport drives its own: pages of 1,000 seeded
+ * newest first, the newest `calibrationMs` calibrating, older rows admitted at
+ * the floor, then live trades recorded one by one, the clock following the tape.
+ */
+function driveBootstrapThenLive(pair: PoolPair, clock: { now: number }, options: { calibrationMs: number; liveFrom: number }): void {
+  const bootstrap = trades.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange < options.liveFrom);
+  const live = trades.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange >= options.liveFrom);
+  clock.now = trades[trades.length - 1].ts_exchange + 1_000;
+  const calibrationFrom = clock.now - options.calibrationMs;
+  for (let end = bootstrap.length; end > 0; end -= 1_000) {
+    const page = bootstrap.slice(Math.max(0, end - 1_000), end);
+    const calibration = page.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange >= calibrationFrom);
+    const older = page.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange < calibrationFrom);
+    const fromMs = page[0].ts_exchange;
+    for (const pool of [pair.runtime, pair.browser]) {
+      if (calibration.length > 0) pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs, calibrate: true });
+      if (older.length > 0) pool.seed(older, { fromMs });
+    }
+    expectSamePool(pair);
+  }
+  live.forEach((trade, index) => {
+    clock.now = Math.max(clock.now, trade.ts_exchange);
+    expect(pair.browser.record(trade)).toBe(pair.runtime.record(trade));
+    if (index % 100 === 0) expectSamePool(pair);
+  });
+  expectSamePool(pair);
+}
+
+describe("tape parity: aggressive-pool.js", () => {
+  const liveFrom = trades[8_000].ts_exchange;
+
+  it("holds the same prints, floor and coverage after a bootstrap and 2,000 live trades (default pool)", () => {
+    const clock = { now: 0 };
+    const now = () => clock.now;
+    const pair: PoolPair = { runtime: createRuntimePool({ now }), browser: createAggressivePool<TradeFrame>({ now }) };
+    driveBootstrapThenLive(pair, clock, { calibrationMs: 15 * 60 * 1000, liveFrom });
+    const snapshot = pair.browser.snapshot();
+    expect(snapshot.prints.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(snapshot.floor).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  });
+
+  it("agrees when only the newest two minutes calibrate and the rest is admitted at the floor", () => {
+    const clock = { now: 0 };
+    const now = () => clock.now;
+    const pair: PoolPair = { runtime: createRuntimePool({ now }), browser: createAggressivePool<TradeFrame>({ now }) };
+    driveBootstrapThenLive(pair, clock, { calibrationMs: 2 * 60 * 1000, liveFrom });
+  });
+
+  it("agrees through the size trim and the time window with a tight pool", () => {
+    const clock = { now: 0 };
+    const now = () => clock.now;
+    const pair: PoolPair = {
+      runtime: createRuntimePool({ now, maxPrints: 300, windowMs: 3 * 60 * 1000 }),
+      browser: createAggressivePool<TradeFrame>({ now, maxPrints: 300, windowMs: 3 * 60 * 1000 }),
+    };
+    driveBootstrapThenLive(pair, clock, { calibrationMs: 60 * 1000, liveFrom });
+    expect(pair.browser.snapshot().prints.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(300);
+  });
+
+  it("agrees when the whole tape is recorded live, with duplicates and a late print", () => {
+    const clock = { now: trades[0].ts_exchange };
+    const now = () => clock.now;
+    const pair: PoolPair = { runtime: createRuntimePool({ now }), browser: createAggressivePool<TradeFrame>({ now }) };
+    trades.forEach((trade, index) => {
+      clock.now = Math.max(clock.now, trade.ts_exchange);
+      expect(pair.browser.record(trade)).toBe(pair.runtime.record(trade));
+      if (index % 1_000 === 999) {
+        expect(pair.browser.record(trade)).toBe(pair.runtime.record(trade));
+        const late = { ...trades[index - 500], trade_id: `late${index}`, size: trades[index - 500].size * 50 };
+        expect(pair.browser.record(late)).toBe(pair.runtime.record(late));
+        expectSamePool(pair);
+      }
+    });
+    expectSamePool(pair);
+  });
+});

+### Tape-derived frames (L1 parity)
+
+The frames the runtime derives from the tape are computed in the worker with
+the runtime's own algorithms. `apps/web/src/lib/tape/largeTradeDetection.ts`
+and `aggressivePool.ts` mirror `src/indicators/large-trade/large-trade-detection.js`
+and `src/server/aggressive-pool.js` (with `print-floor.js` and `floorAbove`)
+line for line, and `lib/chart/markerRetention.ts` is the browser's copy of
+`src/server/history.js`'s per-minute retention;
+`apps/web/src/workers/tests/tapeParity.test.ts` requires the runtime files and
+asserts identical output on 10,000 recorded BTC-USDT aggregate trades
+(`workers/tests/fixtures/binance-mirror/aggtrades-btcusdt-10k.json.gz`).
+
+- `marker`: every live trade goes through the retention (four prints of
+  largest USD notional per minute and side, 100,000 at most, seven days); an
+  admitted print is sent as a `marker` right behind its `trade`, as the
+  runtime does. No `baseline_log_*` fields: the runtime sends none for Binance.
+- Bootstrap: after the first snapshot, alongside the stream, the worker pages
+  `GET /api/v3/aggTrades?symbol=…&limit=1000` from the mirror, newest first by
+  `fromId`, at most 30 pages (weight 4 each) or seven days, one 10 s timeout
+  per page. Rows are aggregate trades, ids `a<aggTradeId>` (a separate id
+  space from the stream's trade ids; a bootstrap print is one taker order at
+  one price, so it can be larger than any single fill the runtime saw). A
+  page is validated whole (every row well formed, ids consecutive, starting
+  at `fromId`): a page with a malformed row is a failed read, retried or
+  ending the walk with the pages before it, never a shorter page that would
+  leave a hole inside claimed coverage. The pages are staged until the
+  stream's first trade is known (or, with a stream connecting or open but
+  silent, for a two-second grace), then every row is reconciled against the
+  live tape by fill id (`f`/`l` against the span's first and last trade id):
+  rows the stream delivered are dropped, an aggregate straddling a span
+  boundary is dropped and its millisecond marked unverified, outside every
+  claim. Then the `snapshot` is re-broadcast
+  (every closed bar held, the last 100 live trades, the newest 5,000 markers,
+  the pool), the forming bar is re-sent, and a client that opted in through
+  `capabilities` gets the full marker set as `marker_history` (`reset` on the
+  first frame, 5,000 rows each). On BTC-USDT 30 pages are about twenty
+  minutes of tape; on a quiet pair, hours. The runtime's baseline is seven
+  days of storage.
+- Observed tape and seams: the transport keeps the stretches of tape it has
+  seen in full (the bootstrap's id-contiguous pages, each stream span from its
+  first to its last live trade, with the ids at their ends) and claims
+  coverage only inside one of them, minus unverified milliseconds. Kline
+  recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the hole between the newest page and
+  the stream's first trade, and the hole between one stream's last trade and
+  the next stream's first, are filled from REST (`aggTrades` by id from the
+  row after the last REST row, else by `startTime` from the hole's last
+  observed millisecond, every row reconciled by fill id; at most 10 pages,
+  three tries); a closed hole merges the stretches, an unclosable one stays a
+  hole outside every claim. The bootstrap's snapshot waits for the seam to
+  the stream to settle; with no stream delivering, it goes out claiming
+  nothing (`aggressive_prints_from` = now) while still carrying the prints.
+  The bookkeeping is pruned to the pool's four-hour retention and bounded.
+- A lost stream after trades: the transport closes itself, exactly as a lost
+  runtime socket reaches the worker, which reports `status: closed` (the
+  tick store ends its live stretch there: `AggressiveTape.endLive`) and
+  reconnects; the pair's markers, pool and coverage are kept across transport
+  instances (four pairs at most), so the connect snapshot carries them, no
+  second bootstrap runs, and the hole is filled from REST or left outside
+  every claim. A stream lost before any trade keeps the internal retry
+  (`RECOVERING`, then `UNAVAILABLE` after five failures), as before.
+- `aggressive_prints`, `aggressive_prints_floor`, `aggressive_prints_from`:
+  the runtime's pool (floor from the 98th percentile of the last 4,096 sizes
+  once 200 are seen, never falling; four hours; 20,000 prints). Live trades
+  `record`; the bootstrap's newest fifteen minutes `seed` with
+  `calibrate: true`, older rows at the floor; `aggressive_prints_from` is the
+  start of the continuous observed stretch that reaches now (never before the
+  pool's window), so `coverageOf` is "thin" over the bootstrap and "all" from
+  connect, and nothing is claimed across an open seam.
+- `aggressive_history`: answered from the pool, `complete: true` only for a
+  window inside one observed stretch, within the pool's window, at or above
+  its floor, 10,000 trades a page with `CAP_REACHED` and a cursor bound to
+  the request;
+  otherwise `complete: false`, `reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE"`, which the
+  client retries three times and then rests.
+- Degraded: when the first page fails three times, the READY health frame
+  carries `reason: "BROWSER_DIRECT_NO_TAPE_HISTORY"` (chip
+  `LIVE (BROWSER_DIRECT_NO_TAPE_HISTORY)`); markers and the pool start at
+  connect; retries continue and a later success publishes the snapshot and
+  restores `BROWSER_DIRECT`. A page failing after others keeps what was read.
+
+| L1 indicator | Reads | Browser-direct |
+|---|---|---|
+| Aggressive Order Flow, Delta Divergence, CVD Divergence, Market Structure Delta, FVG Delta, FVG Boxes, L1 Order Blocks, Volume Profile OHLC | bars via `barSides` | works |
+| Large Trade Detector | `markers`, `marker`, `marker_history`; `trade_count` of the snapshot bars for the top-N% target | works: live markers from connect, bootstrap markers for the pages read; the legend appends ` · partial` while the selection is short of its target |
+| VWAP Bands, Large Trade Bubbles | the tick store (live tape, `aggressive_prints`, `aggressive_history`) | works: "all" from connect, "thin" over the bootstrap |
+| Live Delta Profile, Volume Profile (Ticks) | live trades plus `delta_profile` (every trade per price row) | forming window from connect only; `delta_profile` stays `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE` because the pool is floor-filtered and cannot give a faithful pre-connect profile |
+| VPIN Cloud | `vpin` frames (server study) | unavailable: `vpin_status: "DISABLED"` |
+
 What it does not have: the L2 engine and heatmap, walls, icebergs, exhaustion,
 initiative, candle monitor, live profiles and VPIN (their requests get the
 contract's unavailable reply, so those indicators show their own "needs the
-runtime" state); history past 900 bars; trades from before the page opened;
-the marker pool; a book deeper than 20 levels. Spot only: perpetual pairs are
+runtime" state); history past 900 bars; individual trades from before the page
+opened (the bootstrap is aggregate trades, bounded as above); a book deeper
+than 20 levels. Spot only: perpetual pairs are
 attempted on `fapi`/`fstream` and report
 `BROWSER_DIRECT_PERPETUAL_UNREACHABLE` when the browser cannot reach them;
 dxFeed instruments report `BROWSER_DIRECT_NO_DXFEED`. A dropped spot stream

+/** Parses one REST aggTrades row: { a, p, q, f, l, T, m, M }. */
+export function parseAggTradeRow(raw: unknown): AggTradeRow | null {
+  if (!isRecord(raw)) return null;
+  const price = finiteNumber(raw.p);
+  const size = finiteNumber(raw.q);
+  if (price === undefined || size === undefined || price <= 0 || size <= 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.a) || (raw.a as number) < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.T) || (raw.T as number) < 0 || typeof raw.m !== "boolean") return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.f) || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.l) || (raw.f as number) < 0 || (raw.l as number) < (raw.f as number)) return null;
+  return { a: raw.a as number, p: price, q: size, f: raw.f as number, l: raw.l as number, T: raw.T as number, m: raw.m };
+}
+
+/**
+ * One REST page, validated as a whole: every row well formed, no more rows
+ * than asked, ids consecutive (Binance numbers aggregate trades without gaps
+ * per symbol) and, for a page asked by `fromId`, starting at it. A page that
+ * fails any of this is a failed read, never a shorter page: a dropped row
+ * would otherwise end the walk early and leave a hole inside claimed coverage.
+ */
+export function parseAggTradesPage(body: unknown, fromId: number | null): AggTradeRow[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(body)) throw new Error("aggTrades: body is not an array");
+  if (body.length > TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) throw new Error("aggTrades: more rows than asked");
+  const rows: AggTradeRow[] = [];
+  for (const raw of body) {
+    const row = parseAggTradeRow(raw);
+    if (row === null) throw new Error("aggTrades: malformed row");
+    rows.push(row);
+  }
+  rows.sort((a, b) => a.a - b.a);
+  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
+    if (rows[i].a !== rows[i - 1].a + 1) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not id-contiguous");
+    if (rows[i].T < rows[i - 1].T) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not in time order");
+  }
+  if (fromId !== null && rows.length > 0 && rows[0].a !== fromId) throw new Error("aggTrades: page does not start at fromId");
+  return rows;
+}
+
+/** Merges a closed stretch into the list, joining stretches that overlap or touch (adjacent milliseconds); the ends keep the ids of whichever stretch reaches furthest. */
+function addInterval(intervals: TapeInterval[], added: TapeInterval): void {
+  if (!(added.to >= added.from)) return;
+  let merged: TapeInterval = { ...added };
+  const apart: TapeInterval[] = [];
+  for (const interval of intervals) {
+    if (interval.to + 1 < merged.from || interval.from > merged.to + 1) {
+      apart.push(interval);
+      continue;
+    }
+    const start = interval.from < merged.from || (interval.from === merged.from && merged.startTradeId === undefined) ? interval : merged;
+    const end = interval.to > merged.to || (interval.to === merged.to && merged.endTradeId === undefined && merged.endAggId === undefined) ? interval : merged;
+    merged = { from: start.from, to: end.to, startTradeId: start.startTradeId, endTradeId: end.endTradeId, endAggId: end.endAggId };
+  }
+  apart.push(merged);
+  apart.sort((a, b) => a.from - b.from);
+  intervals.splice(0, intervals.length, ...apart);
+}
+
+/** The stretches with the given milliseconds cut out (an unverified boundary millisecond splits the stretch around it). */
+function subtractMilliseconds(intervals: TapeInterval[], milliseconds: readonly number[]): TapeInterval[] {
+  let result = intervals;
+  for (const ms of milliseconds) {
+    const next: TapeInterval[] = [];
+    for (const interval of result) {
+      if (ms < interval.from || ms > interval.to) {
+        next.push(interval);
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (ms > interval.from) next.push({ from: interval.from, to: ms - 1, startTradeId: interval.startTradeId });
+      if (ms < interval.to) next.push({ from: ms + 1, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId });
+    }
+    result = next;
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A bootstrap print as a trade frame. Aggregate trade ids are a separate
+ * sequence from the stream's trade ids, so they carry an `a` prefix: the two
+ * can never be mistaken for one another, and nothing is counted twice.
+ */
+function aggTradeFrame(selection: Selection, row: AggTradeRow): TradeFrame {
+  return { type: "trade", ...selection, ts_exchange: row.T, price: row.p, size: row.q, side: row.m ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${row.a}` };
+}

+function markerOf(trade: TradeFrame): MarkerFrame {
+  return { ...trade, type: "marker" };

+function markerHistoryRow(marker: MarkerFrame): [number, number, number, "b" | "s", string] {
+  return [marker.ts_exchange, marker.price, marker.size, marker.side === "buy" ? "b" : "s", marker.trade_id];

+function tradeOrder(a: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }, b: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }): number {
+  return a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange || (a.trade_id < b.trade_id ? -1 : a.trade_id > b.trade_id ? 1 : 0);
+}

+/** The runtime's request validation (aggressive-history.js parseAggressiveHistoryRequest), minus the exact-key check the worker already applies. */
+export function parseAggressiveHistoryRequest(msg: Record<string, unknown>, nowMs: number): AggressiveHistoryRequest | null {
+  const { request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromMs, to_ts: toMs, min_size: minSize, cursor } = msg;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(requestId) || (requestId as number) < 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(fromMs) || (fromMs as number) < 0) return null;
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(toMs) || (toMs as number) <= (fromMs as number)) return null;
+  if ((toMs as number) > nowMs + AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_FUTURE_SKEW_MS) return null;
+  if ((toMs as number) - (fromMs as number) > MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS) return null;
+  if (typeof minSize !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(minSize) || minSize < 0) return null;
+  if (cursor !== undefined && (typeof cursor !== "string" || cursor.length === 0 || cursor.length > MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(cursor))) return null;
+  return { requestId: requestId as number, fromMs: fromMs as number, toMs: toMs as number, minSize, cursor: typeof cursor === "string" ? cursor : null };
+}
+
+function base64UrlEncode(text: string): string | null {
+  try {
+    return btoa(text).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+function base64UrlDecode(token: string): string | null {
+  try {
+    const padded = token.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/") + "=".repeat((4 - (token.length % 4)) % 4);
+    return atob(padded);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+/** A page cursor bound to its request (as the runtime's: version, instrument, window, floor, position), so a stale or foreign one is refused. */
+export function encodeHistoryCursor(selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest, last: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }): string | null {
+  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
+  const token = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify({
+    v: HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION, s: selection.symbol, k: scope, f: request.fromMs, t: request.toMs, m: request.minSize, x: last.ts_exchange, i: last.trade_id,
+  }));
+  return token !== null && token.length <= MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH ? token : null;
+}
+
+export function decodeHistoryCursor(token: string, selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest): { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string } | null {
+  const text = base64UrlDecode(token);
+  if (text === null) return null;
+  let decoded: unknown;
+  try {
+    decoded = JSON.parse(text);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (!isRecord(decoded)) return null;
+  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
+  if (
+    decoded.v !== HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION || decoded.s !== selection.symbol || decoded.k !== scope ||
+    decoded.f !== request.fromMs || decoded.t !== request.toMs || decoded.m !== request.minSize ||
+    !Number.isSafeInteger(decoded.x) || (decoded.x as number) < request.fromMs || (decoded.x as number) >= request.toMs ||
+    typeof decoded.i !== "string" || decoded.i.length === 0
+  ) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return { ts_exchange: decoded.x as number, trade_id: decoded.i };
+}

+  /** Keeps the pair's tape state for the next instance or the next selection of the pair; the oldest pair goes when too many are kept. */
+  private keepTapeState(session: Session): void {
+    if (session.selection.provider !== "BINANCE") return;
+    const key = tapeStateKey(session.selection);
+    tapeStates.delete(key);
+    tapeStates.set(key, {
+      markers: session.markers,
+      pool: session.pool,
+      observed: session.observed,
+      liveSpans: session.liveSpans,
+      unfillable: session.unfillable,
+      unverified: session.unverified,
+      calibrationFrom: session.calibrationFrom,
+      recentTrades: session.recentTrades,
+      done: session.tape.done,
+    });
+    while (tapeStates.size > MAX_TAPE_STATES) {
+      const oldest = tapeStates.keys().next().value;
+      if (oldest === undefined) break;
+      tapeStates.delete(oldest);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The upstream stream was lost after delivering trades: the tick store on
+   * the main thread has a live span open that must end where the trades
+   * stopped. A lost runtime socket does exactly that through the worker's
+   * close and reconnect, so this transport closes the same way; the pair's
+   * tape state is kept for the instance the worker creates next, which
+   * reconciles the hole from REST or leaves it outside every claim.
+   */
+  private lostUpstream(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: the stream was lost after delivering trades; closing so the worker reconnects");
+    this.close();
+    const event = typeof CloseEvent === "function"
+      ? new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" })
+      : ({ code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" } as unknown as CloseEvent);
+    this.onclose?.(event);
   }
