+
+  // ---- Tape-derived frames: markers, the aggressive pool, their history ----
+
+  /** Offers a print to the runtime's large-trade retention; an admitted one is broadcast as a `marker` when asked. */
+  private offerMarker(session: Session, trade: TradeFrame, broadcast: boolean): void {
+    const marker = markerOf(trade);
+    if (session.markers.insertAll([marker]) && broadcast) this.deliver(marker);
+  }
+
+  /** The newest markers the runtime would still hold: within its seven-day window, oldest first, the newest 5,000 for a snapshot. */
+  private retainedMarkers(session: Session): MarkerFrame[] {
+    const oldestKept = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
+    return session.markers.snapshot().filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange >= oldestKept);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Forgets coverage older than the pool's retention (nothing older can be
+   * claimed: the pool has no print for it) and bounds every list, so
+   * reconnects over a long session cannot grow them without end. The gap a
+   * seam fill is working on, and the stretches beside it, are kept.
+   */
+  private pruneCoverage(session: Session): void {
+    const cutoff = this.now() - POOL_WINDOW_MS;
+    const active = session.seam.gap;
+    const keepsActive = (interval: TapeInterval): boolean =>
+      active !== null && (interval.to === active.from || interval.from === active.to || (interval.from === active.from && interval.to === active.to));
+    const clip = (interval: TapeInterval): TapeInterval =>
+      interval.from >= cutoff ? interval : { from: cutoff, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId };
+    session.observed = session.observed.filter((interval) => interval.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(interval)).map(clip);
+    session.liveSpans = session.liveSpans.filter((span) => span.to >= cutoff);
+    session.unfillable = session.unfillable.filter((gap) => gap.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(gap));
+    session.unverified = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms >= cutoff);
+    if (session.observed.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.observed.splice(0, session.observed.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.liveSpans.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.liveSpans.splice(0, session.liveSpans.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.unfillable.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.unfillable.splice(0, session.unfillable.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
+    if (session.unverified.length > MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS) session.unverified.splice(0, session.unverified.length - MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Every stretch of tape observed in full: the closed ones, plus the open
+   * stream span reaching into the future while a stream delivers, merged
+   * where they touch, with every unverified boundary millisecond cut out.
+   */
+  private coverageIntervals(session: Session): TapeInterval[] {
+    this.pruneCoverage(session);
+    const intervals = session.observed.map((interval) => ({ ...interval }));
+    if (session.live !== null) addInterval(intervals, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, startTradeId: session.live.firstId });
+    return subtractMilliseconds(intervals, session.unverified);
+  }
+
+  /** Where the stretch of complete tape that reaches now begins, or null while no stream delivers. */
+  private coverageNowFrom(session: Session): number | null {
+    const open = this.coverageIntervals(session).find((interval) => interval.to === Infinity);
+    return open === undefined ? null : open.from;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * What the snapshot may claim: the pool's prints, its floor, and coverage
+   * from the start of the continuous observed stretch that reaches now (never
+   * before the pool's own window), or from now itself, an empty claim, while
+   * no stream delivers or a hole separates the stream from what was read.
+   */
+  private poolCoverage(session: Session): { prints: readonly TradeFrame[]; floor: number; from: number } {
+    const snapshot = session.pool.snapshot();
+    const nowMs = this.now();
+    const from = this.coverageNowFrom(session);
+    return { prints: snapshot.prints, floor: snapshot.floor, from: from === null ? nowMs : Math.min(nowMs, Math.max(snapshot.from, from)) };
+  }
+
+  /** The runtime's snapshot for the pair as this transport knows it: every closed bar, the last 100 live trades, the newest markers, the print pool. */
+  private snapshotFrame(session: Session): Record<string, unknown> {
+    const bars = [...session.closedBars.values()].sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
+    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
+    const pool = this.poolCoverage(session);
+    return {
+      type: "snapshot",
+      ...identityFields(session.selection),
+      bars: bars.map((row) => barFrame(session.selection, row, true, row.reconstructed)),
+      trades: [...session.recentTrades],
+      markers: markers.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS),
+      vpin_status: "DISABLED",
+      ...(session.tape.done || session.observed.length > 0 || session.live !== null
+        ? { aggressive_prints: [...pool.prints], aggressive_prints_floor: pool.floor, aggressive_prints_from: pool.from }
+        : {}),
+    };
+  }
+
+  /** The full marker set as marker_history frames, 5,000 rows each, `reset` on the first, as the runtime's markerHistoryFrames. */
+  private deliverMarkerHistory(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || !this.markerHistoryOptIn) return;
+    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
+    if (markers.length === 0) return;
+    session.markerHistorySeq = 0;
+    for (let start = 0; start < markers.length; start += MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS) {
+      this.deliver({
+        type: "marker_history",
+        ...identityFields(session.selection),
+        reset: start === 0,
+        seq: session.markerHistorySeq,
+        rows: markers.slice(start, start + MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS).map(markerHistoryRow),
+      });
+      session.markerHistorySeq += 1;
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** After the bootstrap or a seam fill: the snapshot again (as the runtime re-broadcasts it after a warm-up chunk), the forming bar, and the marker history for a client that opted in. */
+  private publishTapeState(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    session.seam.publishWhenSettled = false;
+    this.deliver(this.snapshotFrame(session));
+    const forming = session.lastFormingBar;
+    if (!session.recovering && forming !== null && forming.openTime > session.lastClosedOpen) this.deliver(barFrame(session.selection, forming, false, false));
+    this.deliverMarkerHistory(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The runtime answers from storage; this answers from the pool, and only
+   * claims completeness for a window that lies inside one observed stretch
+   * of tape (the bootstrap, a seam fill, a stream span), within the pool's
+   * window, at or above the pool's floor. Anything else, a hole between the
+   * REST pages and the stream or between two streams included, is honestly
+   * STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE, which the client retries three times and then rests.
+   * Pages of 10,000 with a cursor bound to the request, as the contract has it.
+   */
+  private aggressiveHistoryReply(session: Session, msg: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+    const request = parseAggressiveHistoryRequest(msg, this.now());
+    if (request === null) return null;
+    const after = request.cursor === null ? null : decodeHistoryCursor(request.cursor, session.selection, request);
+    if (request.cursor !== null && after === null) {
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: dropped aggressive_history request: cursor does not match the selected history window");
+      return null;
+    }
+    const reply = {
+      type: "aggressive_history",
+      ...identityFields(session.selection),
+      request_id: request.requestId,
+      from_ts: request.fromMs,
+      to_ts: request.toMs,
+      min_size: request.minSize,
+      retry_after_ms: null,
+    };
+    const pool = session.pool.snapshot();
+    const inside = this.coverageIntervals(session).some((interval) => interval.from <= request.fromMs && interval.to >= request.toMs - 1);
+    const covered = inside && request.fromMs >= pool.from && request.minSize >= pool.floor;
+    if (!covered) return { ...reply, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", next_cursor: null, trades: [] };
+    const matching = pool.prints
+      .filter((print) =>
+        print.ts_exchange >= request.fromMs && print.ts_exchange < request.toMs && print.size >= request.minSize &&
+        (after === null || tradeOrder(print, after) > 0))
+      .sort(tradeOrder);
+    const capped = matching.length > MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES;
+    const page = capped ? matching.slice(0, MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES) : matching;
+    const nextCursor = capped ? encodeHistoryCursor(session.selection, request, page[page.length - 1]) : null;
+    if (capped && nextCursor === null) return { ...reply, complete: false, reason: "CONVERSION_INCOMPLETE", next_cursor: null, trades: page };
+    return { ...reply, complete: !capped, reason: capped ? "CAP_REACHED" : null, next_cursor: nextCursor, trades: page };
+  }
+
+  private aggTradesUrl(session: Session, query: { fromId?: number; startTime?: number }): string {
+    const path = session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL" ? "/fapi/v1/aggTrades" : "/api/v3/aggTrades";
+    const params = new URLSearchParams({ symbol: session.binanceSymbol, limit: String(TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) });
+    if (query.fromId !== undefined) params.set("fromId", String(query.fromId));
+    if (query.startTime !== undefined) params.set("startTime", String(query.startTime));
+    return `${session.restBase}${path}?${params.toString()}`;
+  }
+
+  /** One validated page of aggregate trades, oldest first: the newest page, the page from `fromId`, or the page from `startTime` on. */
+  private async fetchAggTrades(session: Session, query: { fromId?: number; startTime?: number }, owner: { abort: AbortController | null }): Promise<AggTradeRow[]> {
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    owner.abort = controller;
+    const timer = this.setTimer(() => controller.abort(), REST_TIMEOUT_MS);
+    try {
+      const response = await this.fetchImpl(this.aggTradesUrl(session, query), { signal: controller.signal });
+      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`aggTrades: HTTP ${response.status}`);
+      return parseAggTradesPage(await response.json(), query.fromId ?? null);
+    } finally {
+      this.clearTimer(timer);
+      if (owner.abort === controller) owner.abort = null;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private startTapeBootstrap(session: Session): void {
+    if (session.tape.started) return;
+    session.tape.started = true;
+    void this.bootstrapTape(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The runtime seeds markers and the pool from storage; the browser reads
+   * recent aggregate trades from the mirror, newest page first by id, within a
+   * bounded page budget. The pages are staged, not seeded, until the stream's
+   * first trade is known (or no stream can deliver one that overlaps them):
+   * every row is then reconciled against the live tape by trade id before it
+   * is fed through the same retention and pool. The pages read are one
+   * id-contiguous stretch, recorded as observed; a page that fails validation
+   * or transport after others succeeded ends the walk with what was read; a
+   * first page that fails is retried with backoff, and after three such
+   * failures the READY health frame says the tape history is missing.
+   */
+  private async bootstrapTape(session: Session): Promise<void> {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    tape.inFlight = true;
+    const oldestWanted = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
+    let fromId: number | null = null;
+    let pages = 0;
+    try {
+      while (pages < MAX_TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGES) {
+        let rows: AggTradeRow[];
+        try {
+          rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? {} : { fromId }, tape);
+        } catch (error) {
+          if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+          if (pages === 0) throw error;
+          console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a trade history page failed; keeping what was read", error);
+          break;
+        }
+        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+        pages += 1;
+        if (rows.length === 0) break;
+        tape.staged.push(rows);
+        const oldest = rows[0];
+        if (rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE || oldest.a === 0 || oldest.T < oldestWanted) break;
+        fromId = Math.max(0, oldest.a - TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE);
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+      tape.inFlight = false;
+      tape.attempts += 1;
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: trade history request failed", error);
+      if (tape.attempts >= TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED && !tape.unavailable) {
+        tape.unavailable = true;
+        if (!session.degraded && !session.recovering) this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", this.readyReason(session));
+      }
+      this.scheduleTapeRetry(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    tape.inFlight = false;
+    tape.done = true;
+    const wasUnavailable = tape.unavailable;
+    tape.unavailable = false;
+    if (wasUnavailable && !session.degraded && !session.recovering) this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", HEALTH_REASON_READY);
+    this.commitStagedWhenSafe(session);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * The staged pages can be reconciled against the live tape only once its
+   * first trade is known. With a stream delivering, or none that could ever
+   * deliver a trade older than the pages (no stream connecting or open), they
+   * commit at once; with a stream connecting or open but silent, a short
+   * grace keeps a first trade that is still in flight from overlapping
+   * already committed rows.
+   */
+  private commitStagedWhenSafe(session: Session): void {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    if (!tape.done || tape.staged.length === 0) return;
+    const streamPending = session.stream !== null && session.stream.readyState <= OPEN;
+    if (session.live === null && session.liveSpans.length === 0 && streamPending) {
+      if (tape.graceTimer !== null) return;
+      tape.graceTimer = this.setTimer(() => {
+        tape.graceTimer = null;
+        if (this.isCurrent(session)) this.commitStaged(session);
+      }, BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (tape.graceTimer !== null) {
+      this.clearTimer(tape.graceTimer);
+      tape.graceTimer = null;
+    }
+    this.commitStaged(session);
+  }
+
+  private commitStaged(session: Session): void {
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    const staged = tape.staged;
+    tape.staged = [];
+    let kept = 0;
+    let from = Infinity;
+    let to = -Infinity;
+    let endAggId: number | undefined;
+    for (const page of staged) {
+      kept += this.seedTape(session, page);
+      for (const row of page) {
+        if (row.T < from) from = row.T;
+        if (row.T > to) to = row.T;
+        if (endAggId === undefined || row.a > endAggId) endAggId = row.a;
+      }
+    }
+    if (from <= to) addInterval(session.observed, { from, to, endAggId });
+    if (kept > 0 || from <= to) {
+      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
+      this.fillNextSeam(session);
+    }
+  }
+
+  private scheduleTapeRetry(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+    const tape = session.tape;
+    if (tape.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(tape.timer);
+    const delay = tape.backoffMs;
+    tape.backoffMs = Math.min(tape.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
+    tape.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
+      tape.timer = null;
+      if (this.isCurrent(session)) void this.bootstrapTape(session);
+    }, delay);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Whether a REST row is tape the stream already delivered. Inside a live
+   * span (open or closed) it is; at a span's first or last millisecond the
+   * aggregate's fill ids decide: wholly before the first live trade or wholly
+   * after the last one it is missing tape and is kept, wholly within it is
+   * dropped, and an aggregate straddling the boundary cannot be told apart,
+   * so it is dropped and that millisecond is marked unverified: no coverage
+   * claim includes it.
+   */
+  private admitRestRow(session: Session, row: AggTradeRow): boolean {
+    const spans: readonly LiveSpan[] = session.live === null
+      ? session.liveSpans
+      : [...session.liveSpans, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, firstId: session.live.firstId, lastId: Infinity }];
+    for (const span of spans) {
+      if (row.T > span.from && row.T < span.to) return false;
+      if (row.T === span.from) {
+        if (row.l < span.firstId) continue;
+        if (row.f >= span.firstId) return false;
+        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
+        return false;
+      }
+      if (row.T === span.to) {
+        if (row.f > span.lastId) continue;
+        if (row.l <= span.lastId) return false;
+        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  private markUnverified(session: Session, ms: number): void {
+    if (!session.unverified.includes(ms)) session.unverified.push(ms);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * One validated page into the retention and the pool, as the runtime's
+   * warm-up seeds a storage chunk: rows from the calibration window (the
+   * newest fifteen minutes at the bootstrap's start) calibrate the floor
+   * (`calibrate: true`, every size counted), older rows are admitted at the
+   * floor; rows the stream already delivered are left out. Returns the rows kept.
+   */
+  private seedTape(session: Session, rows: readonly AggTradeRow[]): number {
+    const calibration: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    const older: TradeFrame[] = [];
+    let oldestTs = Infinity;
+    for (const row of rows) {
+      if (!this.admitRestRow(session, row)) continue;
+      const trade = aggTradeFrame(session.selection, row);
+      if (row.T < oldestTs) oldestTs = row.T;
+      (row.T >= session.calibrationFrom ? calibration : older).push(trade);
+      this.offerMarker(session, trade, false);
+    }
+    if (oldestTs === Infinity) return 0;
+    if (calibration.length > 0) session.pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs: oldestTs, calibrate: true });
+    if (older.length > 0) session.pool.seed(older, { fromMs: oldestTs });
+    return calibration.length + older.length;
+  }
+
+  /** The newest hole between two observed stretches (the open stream span counts) that is neither given up on nor made only of unverified milliseconds. */
+  private nextSeamGap(session: Session): TapeInterval | null {
+    const intervals = this.coverageIntervals(session);
+    for (let i = intervals.length - 1; i >= 1; i -= 1) {
+      const gap: TapeInterval = { from: intervals[i - 1].to, to: intervals[i].from, endAggId: intervals[i - 1].endAggId, endTradeId: intervals[i - 1].endTradeId };
+      if (session.unfillable.some((open) => open.from === gap.from && open.to === gap.to)) continue;
+      const interior = gap.to - gap.from - 1;
+      const unverifiedInside = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms > gap.from && ms < gap.to).length;
+      if (interior <= unverifiedInside) continue;
+      return gap;
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Closes the holes the tape has: between the bootstrap's newest row and the
+   * stream's first trade, or between one stream's last trade and the next
+   * stream's first. Kline recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the prints of
+   * such a hole are read from REST within a bounded budget: by id from the
+   * row after the last REST row when the hole follows one, else by time from
+   * the hole's first millisecond, every row reconciled against the live tape
+   * by fill id. A hole that cannot be closed stays a hole and is never inside
+   * a completeness claim. One fill runs at a time; when nothing is left to
+   * fill and a snapshot is waiting, it goes out.
+   */
+  private fillNextSeam(session: Session): void {
+    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || session.seam.inFlight || session.seam.timer !== null) return;
+    const gap = this.nextSeamGap(session);
+    if (gap === null) {
+      if (session.seam.publishWhenSettled) this.publishTapeState(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    void this.fillSeam(session, gap);
+  }
+
+  private async fillSeam(session: Session, gap: TapeInterval): Promise<void> {
+    const seam = session.seam;
+    seam.inFlight = true;
+    seam.gap = gap;
+    let closed = false;
+    let endAggId = gap.endAggId;
+    try {
+      let fromId: number | null = gap.endAggId === undefined ? null : gap.endAggId + 1;
+      for (let pages = 0; pages < MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES; pages += 1) {
+        const rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? { startTime: gap.from } : { fromId }, seam);
+        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+        const inside = rows.filter((row) => row.T <= gap.to);
+        if (inside.length > 0) {
+          this.seedTape(session, inside);
+          endAggId = inside[inside.length - 1].a;
+        }
+        // The hole is closed when a row past its end was read, or the page
+        // was short (nothing newer existed on the exchange at the read).
+        if (inside.length < rows.length || rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) {
+          closed = true;
+          break;
+        }
+        fromId = rows[rows.length - 1].a + 1;
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
+      seam.inFlight = false;
+      seam.gap = null;
+      seam.failures += 1;
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam fill failed", error);
+      if (seam.failures < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP) {
+        const delay = seam.backoffMs;
+        seam.backoffMs = Math.min(seam.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
+        seam.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
+          seam.timer = null;
+          this.fillNextSeam(session);
+        }, delay);
+        return;
+      }
+      seam.failures = 0;
+      seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
+      this.fillNextSeam(session);
+      return;
+    }
+    seam.inFlight = false;
+    seam.gap = null;
+    seam.failures = 0;
+    seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
+    if (closed) {
+      addInterval(session.observed, { from: gap.from, to: gap.to, endAggId });
+      // Coverage grew: the snapshot says so.
+      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
+    } else {
+      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam is wider than a fill may walk; it stays open");
+      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
+    }
+    this.fillNextSeam(session);
+  }
 }

   589	export class BrowserBinanceTransport implements TransportSocket {
   590	  readyState = CONNECTING;
   591	  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   592	  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
   593	  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
   594	  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   595	
   596	  readonly url: string;
   597	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch;
   598	  private readonly WebSocketImpl: new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket;
   599	  private readonly now: () => number;
   600	  private readonly setTimer: (callback: () => void, ms: number) => unknown;
   601	  private readonly clearTimer: (handle: unknown) => void;
   602	  private readonly endpoints: BrowserBinanceEndpoints;
   603	  private session: Session | null = null;
   604	  private openTimer: unknown = null;
   605	  /** The worker opted in to marker_history frames (its `capabilities` message), as the runtime remembers per client. */
   606	  private markerHistoryOptIn = false;
   607	
   608	  constructor(url: string, options: BrowserBinanceTransportOptions = {}) {
   609	    this.url = url;
   610	    this.fetchImpl = options.fetch ?? ((input, init) => fetch(input, init));
   611	    this.WebSocketImpl = options.WebSocket ?? (WebSocket as unknown as new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket);
   612	    this.now = options.now ?? (() => Date.now());
   613	    this.setTimer = options.setTimeout ?? ((callback, ms) => setTimeout(callback, ms));
   614	    this.clearTimer = options.clearTimeout ?? ((handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>));
   615	    this.endpoints = { ...DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS, ...(options.endpoints ?? {}) };
   616	    // A WebSocket opens asynchronously; so does this, so the worker installs
   617	    // its handlers before anything fires.
   618	    this.openTimer = this.setTimer(() => {
   619	      this.openTimer = null;
   620	      if (this.readyState !== CONNECTING) return;
   621	      this.readyState = OPEN;
   622	      this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
   623	      // The runtime pushes its current pair on connect; with no selection
   624	      // from the worker we do the same with the catalog's first spot pair.
   625	      if (this.session === null && this.readyState === OPEN) this.select(defaultSelection());
   626	    }, 0);
   627	  }
   628	
   629	  /** Closed bars held back while a gap fill is pending (diagnostics and tests). */
   630	  get heldClosedBarCount(): number {
   631	    return this.session?.heldClosedBars.size ?? 0;
   632	  }
   633	
   634	  /** Sizes of the tape coverage bookkeeping (diagnostics and tests). */
   635	  get tapeCoverage(): { observed: number; liveSpans: number; unfillable: number; unverified: number; staged: number } {
   636	    const session = this.session;
   637	    if (session === null) return { observed: 0, liveSpans: 0, unfillable: 0, unverified: 0, staged: 0 };
   638	    return { observed: session.observed.length, liveSpans: session.liveSpans.length, unfillable: session.unfillable.length, unverified: session.unverified.length, staged: session.tape.staged.length };
   639	  }
   640	
   641	  send(data: string): void {
   642	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) throw new DOMException("transport is not open", "InvalidStateError");
   643	    let msg: unknown;
   644	    try {
   645	      msg = JSON.parse(data);
   646	    } catch {
   647	      return;
   648	    }
   649	    if (!isRecord(msg) || typeof msg.type !== "string") return;
   650	    if (msg.type === "select") {
   651	      const selection = parseSelectRequest(msg);
   652	      if (selection !== null) this.select(selection);
   653	      return;
   654	    }
   655	    if (msg.type === "capabilities") {
   656	      // As the runtime: opting in gets the current marker set at once (nothing
   657	      // when there is none yet; the bootstrap sends it when it completes).
   658	      const optIn = msg.marker_history === true;
   659	      const session = this.session;
   660	      this.markerHistoryOptIn = optIn;
   661	      if (optIn && session !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliverMarkerHistory(session), 0);
   662	      return;
   663	    }
   664	    const reply = this.replyFor(msg);
   665	    if (reply !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliver(reply), 0);
   666	  }
   667	
   668	  close(): void {
   669	    if (this.readyState === CLOSED) return;
   670	    this.readyState = CLOSED;
   671	    if (this.openTimer !== null) {
   672	      this.clearTimer(this.openTimer);
   673	      this.openTimer = null;
   674	    }
   675	    this.endSession();
   676	  }
   677	
   678	  /** The contract's own "not available" answer for a request this transport cannot serve; null when the contract defines none. */
   679	  private replyFor(msg: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | null {
   680	    const session = this.session;
   681	    if (session === null) return null;
   682	    const identity = identityFields(session.selection);
   683	    const echo = (keys: readonly string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null => {
   684	      const fields: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   685	      for (const key of keys) {
   686	        if (msg[key] === undefined) return null;
   687	        fields[key] = msg[key];
   688	      }
   689	      return fields;
   690	    };
   691	    switch (msg.type) {
   692	      case "aggressive_history":
   693	        return this.aggressiveHistoryReply(session, msg);
   694	      case "delta_profile": {
   695	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "row_width"]);
   696	        if (fields === null) return null;
   697	        return { type: "delta_profile", ...identity, ...fields, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, pending: 0, rows: [] };
   698	      }
   699	      case "band_history": {
   700	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi", "column_ms"]);
   701	        if (fields === null) return null;
   702	        return { type: "band_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, row_lo: null, row_hi: null, complete: false, reason: "GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, columns: [] };
   703	      }
   704	      case "wall_history": {
   705	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   706	        if (fields === null) return null;
   707	        return { type: "wall_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, walls: [], next: null };
   708	      }
   709	      case "l2_event_history": {
   710	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   711	        if (fields === null) return null;
   712	        return { type: "l2_event_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, trades: [], wall_trades: [], trades_held_from: null, next: null };
   713	      }
   714	      case "profile_config":
   715	      case "profile_history":
   716	        return { type: "analytics_status", ...identity, indicator: "PROFILE", status: "UNAVAILABLE" };
   717	      default:
   718	        // capabilities, liquidity_resync, l2_resync, exhaustion_history,
   719	        // initiative_history: the contract defines no reply shape, so the
   720	        // indicators keep their own "no frame yet" state.
   721	        return null;
   722	    }
   723	  }
   724	
   725	  private deliver(frame: Record<string, unknown>): void {
   726	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) return;
   727	    const data = JSON.stringify(frame);
   728	    const event = typeof MessageEvent === "function"
   729	      ? new MessageEvent("message", { data })
   730	      : ({ data } as unknown as MessageEvent);
   731	    this.onmessage?.(event);
   732	  }
   733	
   734	  private endSession(): void {
   735	    const session = this.session;
   736	    if (session === null) return;
   737	    this.session = null;
   738	    session.closed = true;
   739	    session.abort?.abort();
   740	    session.abort = null;
   741	    if (session.timer !== null) {
   742	      this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   743	      session.timer = null;
   744	    }
   745	    session.tape.abort?.abort();
   746	    session.tape.abort = null;
   747	    if (session.tape.timer !== null) {
   748	      this.clearTimer(session.tape.timer);
   749	      session.tape.timer = null;
   750	    }
   751	    session.seam.abort?.abort();
   752	    session.seam.abort = null;
   753	    if (session.seam.timer !== null) {
   754	      this.clearTimer(session.seam.timer);
   755	      session.seam.timer = null;
   756	    }
   757	    if (session.tape.graceTimer !== null) {
   758	      this.clearTimer(session.tape.graceTimer);
   759	      session.tape.graceTimer = null;
   760	    }
   761	    this.closeStream(session);
   762	    this.keepTapeState(session);
   763	  }
   764	
   765	  /** Keeps the pair's tape state for the next instance or the next selection of the pair; the oldest pair goes when too many are kept. */
   766	  private keepTapeState(session: Session): void {
   767	    if (session.selection.provider !== "BINANCE") return;
   768	    const key = tapeStateKey(session.selection);
   769	    tapeStates.delete(key);
   770	    tapeStates.set(key, {
   771	      markers: session.markers,
   772	      pool: session.pool,
   773	      observed: session.observed,
   774	      liveSpans: session.liveSpans,
   775	      unfillable: session.unfillable,
   776	      unverified: session.unverified,
   777	      calibrationFrom: session.calibrationFrom,
   778	      recentTrades: session.recentTrades,
   779	      done: session.tape.done,
   780	    });
   781	    while (tapeStates.size > MAX_TAPE_STATES) {
   782	      const oldest = tapeStates.keys().next().value;
   783	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
   784	      tapeStates.delete(oldest);
   785	    }
   786	  }
   787	
   788	  /**
   789	   * The upstream stream was lost after delivering trades: the tick store on
   790	   * the main thread has a live span open that must end where the trades
   791	   * stopped. A lost runtime socket does exactly that through the worker's
   792	   * close and reconnect, so this transport closes the same way; the pair's
   793	   * tape state is kept for the instance the worker creates next, which
   794	   * reconciles the hole from REST or leaves it outside every claim.
   795	   */
   796	  private lostUpstream(session: Session): void {
   797	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   798	    console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: the stream was lost after delivering trades; closing so the worker reconnects");
   799	    this.close();
   800	    const event = typeof CloseEvent === "function"
   801	      ? new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" })
   802	      : ({ code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" } as unknown as CloseEvent);
   803	    this.onclose?.(event);
   804	  }
   805	
   806	  private closeStream(session: Session): void {
   807	    if (session.connectTimer !== null) {
   808	      this.clearTimer(session.connectTimer);
   809	      session.connectTimer = null;
   810	    }
   811	    // The tape this stream delivered is observed up to its last live trade;
   812	    // the prints between that and the next stream's first trade are a hole,
   813	    // which a seam fill closes from REST or which stays open.
   814	    const live = session.live;
   815	    if (live !== null) {
   816	      session.liveSpans.push({ from: live.from, to: live.last, firstId: live.firstId, lastId: live.lastId });
   817	      addInterval(session.observed, { from: live.from, to: live.last, startTradeId: live.firstId, endTradeId: live.lastId });
   818	      session.live = null;
   819	    }
   820	    const stream = session.stream;
   821	    session.stream = null;
   822	    if (stream === null) return;
   823	    stream.onopen = null;
   824	    stream.onmessage = null;
   825	    stream.onclose = null;
   826	    stream.onerror = null;
   827	    try {
   828	      stream.close();
   829	    } catch {
   830	      void 0;
  1080	      // A gap fill in flight belongs to the stream that just died: abandon it.
  1081	      session.abort?.abort();
  1082	      session.awaitingBoundary = null;
  1083	      if (deliveredTrades) {
  1084	        this.lostUpstream(session);
  1085	        return;
  1086	      }
  1087	      this.streamLost(session);
  1088	    };
  1089	    stream.onerror = () => {
  1090	      // The browser follows every error with a close event; that path reports it.
  1091	    };
  1092	  }
  1093	
  1094	  private streamLost(session: Session): void {
  1095	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
  1096	    if (session.timer !== null) {
  1097	      this.clearTimer(session.timer);
  1098	      session.timer = null;
  1099	    }
  1100	    session.streamFailures += 1;
  1101	    session.degraded = true;
  1102	    if (session.bookLive) {
  1103	      // The last READY book is the dead stream's; consumers read the book's own status.
  1104	      session.bookLive = false;
  1105	      this.deliver({ type: "book", ...identityFields(session.selection), status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: HEALTH_REASON_STREAM_CLOSED });
  1106	    }
  1107	    if (session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL") {
  1108	      // No mirror exists for the futures stream: say so at once, then keep trying.
  1109	      this.deliverHealth(session, "UNAVAILABLE", HEALTH_REASON_PERPETUAL_UNREACHABLE);
  1110	    } else if (session.streamFailures >= STREAM_FAILURES_BEFORE_UNAVAILABLE) {
  1111	      this.deliverHealth(session, "UNAVAILABLE", HEALTH_REASON_STREAM_UNAVAILABLE);
  1112	    } else {
  1113	      this.deliverHealth(session, "RECOVERING", HEALTH_REASON_STREAM_CLOSED);
  1114	    }
  1115	    this.scheduleRetry(session, () => this.openStream(session));
  1116	  }
  1117	
  1118	  private recoveryIsLive(session: Session, generation: number): boolean {
  1119	    return this.isCurrent(session) && session.streamGeneration === generation && session.stream !== null && session.recovering;
  1120	  }
  1121	
  1122	  private earliestHeldClose(session: Session): number | undefined {
  1123	    let earliest: number | undefined;
  1124	    for (const openTime of session.heldClosedBars.keys()) {
  1125	      if (earliest === undefined || openTime < earliest) earliest = openTime;
  1126	    }
  1127	    return earliest;
  1128	  }
  1129	
  1130	  /**
  1131	   * Whenever a stream opens: the closed bars between the last confirmed close
  1132	   * and the live stream come from REST, marked reconstructed as the runtime
  1133	   * marks its backfill. Pages of 1,000 minutes are walked from that boundary;
  1134	   * every row must be the next consecutive minute, and a row counts as final
  1135	   * only when its minute closed before the request went out (a margin covers
  1136	   * clock skew), or when the stream itself closed that minute meanwhile (its
  1137	   * close wins). Closes the new stream reports during the fill wait in the
  1138	   * buffer so the main thread sees closed bars in order and never a hole. The
  1139	   * fill ends at the minute now forming, or at a minute that closed on the
  1140	   * clock but is not yet confirmable, which the stream's own close or a later
  1141	   * re-read settles. A missing, short or failed page keeps the feed RECOVERING
  1142	   * and is retried from the first missing minute; a gap longer than the pages
  1143	   * allowed, or a minute the exchange keeps omitting, is re-read as a fresh
  1144	   * snapshot instead. READY follows only a continuous fill for the stream
  1145	   * that is still open.
  1146	   */
  1147	  private async recoverAfterGap(session: Session, generation: number): Promise<void> {
  1148	    session.awaitingBoundary = null;
  1149	    try {
  1150	      for (let page = 1; ; page += 1) {
  1151	        if (page > MAX_GAP_FILL_PAGES || (session.lastClosedOpen >= 0 && this.now() - session.lastClosedOpen > MAX_GAP_FILL_MS)) {
  1152	          this.restartFromSnapshot(session);
  1153	          return;
  1154	        }
  1155	        const paged = session.lastClosedOpen >= 0;
  1156	        const confirmedBefore = this.now() - CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS;
  1157	        session.fillInFlight = true;
  1158	        let rows: KlineRow[];
  1159	        try {
  1160	          rows = await this.fetchKlines(session, paged ? { startTime: session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS, limit: GAP_FILL_PAGE } : { limit: SNAPSHOT_BARS + 1 });
  1161	        } finally {
  1162	          session.fillInFlight = false;
  1163	        }
  1164	        if (!this.recoveryIsLive(session, generation)) return;
  1165	        const now = this.now();
  1166	        let expected = paged ? session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS : rows.length > 0 ? rows[0].openTime : -1;
  1167	        let outcome: "continuous" | "hole" | "forming" | "boundary" = "continuous";
  1168	        for (const row of rows) {
  1169	          if (row.openTime < expected) continue;
  1170	          if (row.openTime > expected) {
  1171	            outcome = "hole";
  1172	            break;
  1173	          }
  1174	          const held = session.heldClosedBars.get(row.openTime);
  1175	          if (held !== undefined) {
  1176	            // The stream closed this minute itself: its close is authoritative.
  1177	            this.deliverClosedBar(session, held, false);
  1178	          } else if (row.closeTime < confirmedBefore) {
  1179	            this.deliverClosedBar(session, row, true);
  1180	          } else {
  1181	            outcome = row.closeTime < now ? "boundary" : "forming";
  1182	            break;
  1183	          }
  1184	          expected = row.openTime + BAR_INTERVAL_MS;
  1185	        }
  1186	        if (outcome === "hole") {
  1187	          session.holeRetries += 1;
  1188	          if (session.holeRetries > MAX_HOLE_RETRIES) {
  1189	            // The exchange keeps omitting this minute: a real gap, carried as a gap.
  1190	            this.restartFromSnapshot(session);
  1191	            return;
  1192	          }
  1193	          throw new Error(`gap fill: the exchange skipped the minute at ${expected}`);
  1194	        }
  1195	        session.holeRetries = 0;
  1196	        if (outcome === "boundary") {
  1197	          // The minute just closed on the clock but the row may be partial.
  1198	          // The stream's own close for it ends the recovery as soon as it
  1199	          // arrives; failing that, it is re-read once it is confirmable.
  1200	          session.awaitingBoundary = expected;
  1201	          const retryAt = expected + BAR_INTERVAL_MS + CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS;
  1202	          this.scheduleRetryAt(session, Math.max(retryAt - now, STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS), () => {
  1203	            if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, generation)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, generation);
  1204	          });
  1205	          return;
  1206	        }
  1207	        if (outcome === "forming") break;
  1208	        if (paged && rows.length >= GAP_FILL_PAGE) continue;
  1209	        const earliestHeld = this.earliestHeldClose(session);
  1210	        if (!paged || earliestHeld === expected) break;
  1211	        throw new Error("gap fill: the exchange returned fewer rows than the gap spans");
  1212	      }
  1213	    } catch (error) {
  1214	      if (!this.recoveryIsLive(session, generation)) return;
  1215	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: kline gap fill failed", error);
  1216	      session.degraded = true;
  1217	      this.deliverHealth(session, "RECOVERING", HEALTH_REASON_REST_FAILED);
  1218	      this.scheduleRetry(session, () => {
  1219	        if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, generation)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, generation);
  1220	      });
  1221	      return;
  1222	    }
  1223	    if (!this.finishRecovery(session)) {
  1224	      // Held closes are not consecutive with the boundary: the minutes between
  1225	      // are read next, from the first missing one.
  1226	      session.degraded = true;
  1227	      this.deliverHealth(session, "RECOVERING", HEALTH_REASON_REST_FAILED);
  1228	      this.scheduleRetry(session, () => {
  1229	        if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, generation)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, generation);
  1230	      });
  1231	    }
  1232	  }
  1233	
  1234	  /** Releases held closes while each is the next minute, then the latest held forming bar; true when the recovery is complete. */
  1235	  private finishRecovery(session: Session): boolean {
  1236	    for (const row of [...session.heldClosedBars.values()].sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime)) {
  1237	      if (row.openTime <= session.lastClosedOpen) {
  1238	        session.heldClosedBars.delete(row.openTime);
  1239	        continue;
  1240	      }
  1241	      if (row.openTime !== session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS) return false;
  1242	      this.deliverClosedBar(session, row, false);
  1243	      session.heldClosedBars.delete(row.openTime);
  1244	    }
  1245	    const forming = session.heldFormingBar;
  1246	    session.heldFormingBar = null;
  1247	    if (forming !== null && forming.openTime > session.lastClosedOpen) this.deliverFormingBar(session, forming);
  1248	    session.recovering = false;
  1249	    session.awaitingBoundary = null;
  1250	    session.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1251	    if (session.degraded) {
  1252	      session.degraded = false;
  1253	      this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", this.readyReason(session));
  1254	    }
  1255	    return true;
  1256	  }
  1257	
  1258	  /** A gap too long to page through: close the stream and read the history afresh, as a new selection would. */
  1259	  private restartFromSnapshot(session: Session): void {
  1260	    console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: the gap exceeds what a fill can page through; re-reading the history");
  1261	    this.closeStream(session);
  1262	    session.recovering = false;
  1263	    session.awaitingBoundary = null;
  1264	    session.holeRetries = 0;
  1265	    session.heldClosedBars.clear();
  1266	    session.heldFormingBar = null;
  1267	    void this.bootstrap(session);
  1268	  }
  1269	
  1270	  private deliverClosedBar(session: Session, bar: KlineRow, reconstructed: boolean): void {
  1271	    this.deliver(barFrame(session.selection, bar, true, reconstructed));
  1272	    session.lastClosedOpen = bar.openTime;
  1273	    session.closedBars.set(bar.openTime, { ...bar, reconstructed });
  1274	    while (session.closedBars.size > MAX_SNAPSHOT_CLOSED_BARS) {
  1275	      let oldest: number | undefined;
  1276	      for (const openTime of session.closedBars.keys()) if (oldest === undefined || openTime < oldest) oldest = openTime;
  1277	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
  1278	      session.closedBars.delete(oldest);
  1279	    }
  1280	  }
  1281	
  1282	  private routeStreamMessage(session: Session, parsed: unknown): void {
  1283	    if (!isRecord(parsed)) return;
  1284	    const payload = isRecord(parsed.data) && typeof parsed.stream === "string" ? parsed.data : parsed;
  1285	    if (payload.e === "kline") {
  1286	      const bar = parseKlineEvent(payload.k);
  1287	      if (bar === null) return;
  1288	      if (bar.closed && bar.openTime <= session.lastClosedOpen) return;
  1289	      if (session.recovering) {
  1290	        if (bar.closed && !session.fillInFlight && session.awaitingBoundary === bar.openTime && bar.openTime === session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS) {
  1291	          // The minute the fill could not yet confirm: the stream's close is final.
  1292	          if (session.timer !== null) {
  1293	            this.clearTimer(session.timer);
  1294	            session.timer = null;
  1295	          }
  1296	          this.deliverClosedBar(session, bar, false);
  1297	          if (!this.finishRecovery(session)) {
  1298	            session.degraded = true;
  1299	            this.deliverHealth(session, "RECOVERING", HEALTH_REASON_REST_FAILED);
  1300	            this.scheduleRetry(session, () => {
  1301	              if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, session.streamGeneration)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, session.streamGeneration);
  1302	            });
  1303	          }
  1304	          return;
  1305	        }
  1306	        if (bar.closed) {
  1307	          session.heldClosedBars.set(bar.openTime, bar);
  1308	          // Bounded: a dropped old close is still a closed minute on the
  1309	          // exchange, and the paginated REST read walks up to the earliest
  1310	          // retained one, so nothing is lost.
  1311	          if (session.heldClosedBars.size > MAX_HELD_CLOSED_BARS) {
  1312	            const earliest = this.earliestHeldClose(session);
  1313	            if (earliest !== undefined) session.heldClosedBars.delete(earliest);
  1314	          }
  1315	        } else session.heldFormingBar = bar;
  1316	        return;
  1317	      }
  1318	      if (bar.closed) this.deliverClosedBar(session, bar, false);
  1319	      else this.deliverFormingBar(session, bar);
  1320	      return;

   916	  private klinesUrl(session: Session, query: Record<string, string | number>): string {
   917	    const path = session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL" ? "/fapi/v1/klines" : "/api/v3/klines";
   918	    const params = new URLSearchParams({ symbol: session.binanceSymbol, interval: "1m" });
   919	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query)) params.set(key, String(value));
   920	    return `${session.restBase}${path}?${params.toString()}`;
   921	  }

   923	  private async fetchKlines(session: Session, query: Record<string, string | number>): Promise<KlineRow[]> {
   924	    const controller = new AbortController();
   925	    session.abort = controller;
   926	    const timer = this.setTimer(() => controller.abort(), REST_TIMEOUT_MS);
   927	    try {
   928	      const response = await this.fetchImpl(this.klinesUrl(session, query), { signal: controller.signal });
   929	      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`klines: HTTP ${response.status}`);
   930	      const body: unknown = await response.json();
   931	      if (!Array.isArray(body)) throw new Error("klines: body is not an array");
   932	      const rows: KlineRow[] = [];
   933	      for (const raw of body) {
   934	        const row = parseKlineRow(raw);
   935	        if (row !== null) rows.push(row);
   936	      }
   937	      rows.sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
   938	      return rows;
   939	    } finally {
   940	      this.clearTimer(timer);
   941	      if (session.abort === controller) session.abort = null;
   942	    }
   943	  }
   944	
   945	  private scheduleRetry(session: Session, action: () => void): void {
   946	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   947	    if (session.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   948	    const delay = session.backoffMs;
   949	    session.backoffMs = Math.min(session.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
   950	    session.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
   951	      session.timer = null;
   952	      if (this.isCurrent(session)) action();
   953	    }, delay);
   954	  }

   956	  private scheduleRetryAt(session: Session, delayMs: number, action: () => void): void {
   957	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;

   959	    session.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
   960	      session.timer = null;
   961	      if (this.isCurrent(session)) action();

   971	  private async bootstrap(session: Session): Promise<void> {
   972	    let rows: KlineRow[];
   973	    const confirmedBefore = this.now() - CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS;
   974	    try {

   976	    } catch (error) {

  1321	    }

  1324	      const size = finiteNumber(payload.q);
  1325	      if (price === undefined || size === undefined || price <= 0 || size <= 0) return;

  1376	  // ---- Tape-derived frames: markers, the aggressive pool, their history ----
  1377	
  1378	  /** Offers a print to the runtime's large-trade retention; an admitted one is broadcast as a `marker` when asked. */
  1379	  private offerMarker(session: Session, trade: TradeFrame, broadcast: boolean): void {
  1380	    const marker = markerOf(trade);
  1381	    if (session.markers.insertAll([marker]) && broadcast) this.deliver(marker);
  1382	  }
  1383	
  1384	  /** The newest markers the runtime would still hold: within its seven-day window, oldest first, the newest 5,000 for a snapshot. */
  1385	  private retainedMarkers(session: Session): MarkerFrame[] {
  1386	    const oldestKept = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
  1387	    return session.markers.snapshot().filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange >= oldestKept);
  1388	  }
  1389	
  1390	  /**
  1391	   * Forgets coverage older than the pool's retention (nothing older can be
  1392	   * claimed: the pool has no print for it) and bounds every list, so
  1393	   * reconnects over a long session cannot grow them without end. The gap a
  1394	   * seam fill is working on, and the stretches beside it, are kept.
  1395	   */
  1396	  private pruneCoverage(session: Session): void {
  1397	    const cutoff = this.now() - POOL_WINDOW_MS;
  1398	    const active = session.seam.gap;
  1399	    const keepsActive = (interval: TapeInterval): boolean =>
  1400	      active !== null && (interval.to === active.from || interval.from === active.to || (interval.from === active.from && interval.to === active.to));
  1401	    const clip = (interval: TapeInterval): TapeInterval =>
  1402	      interval.from >= cutoff ? interval : { from: cutoff, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId };
  1403	    session.observed = session.observed.filter((interval) => interval.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(interval)).map(clip);
  1404	    session.liveSpans = session.liveSpans.filter((span) => span.to >= cutoff);
  1405	    session.unfillable = session.unfillable.filter((gap) => gap.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(gap));
  1406	    session.unverified = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms >= cutoff);
  1407	    if (session.observed.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.observed.splice(0, session.observed.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1408	    if (session.liveSpans.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.liveSpans.splice(0, session.liveSpans.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1409	    if (session.unfillable.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.unfillable.splice(0, session.unfillable.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1410	    if (session.unverified.length > MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS) session.unverified.splice(0, session.unverified.length - MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS);
  1411	  }
  1412	
  1413	  /**
  1414	   * Every stretch of tape observed in full: the closed ones, plus the open
  1415	   * stream span reaching into the future while a stream delivers, merged
  1416	   * where they touch, with every unverified boundary millisecond cut out.
  1417	   */
  1418	  private coverageIntervals(session: Session): TapeInterval[] {
  1419	    this.pruneCoverage(session);
  1420	    const intervals = session.observed.map((interval) => ({ ...interval }));
  1421	    if (session.live !== null) addInterval(intervals, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, startTradeId: session.live.firstId });
  1422	    return subtractMilliseconds(intervals, session.unverified);
  1423	  }
  1424	
  1425	  /** Where the stretch of complete tape that reaches now begins, or null while no stream delivers. */
  1426	  private coverageNowFrom(session: Session): number | null {
  1427	    const open = this.coverageIntervals(session).find((interval) => interval.to === Infinity);
  1428	    return open === undefined ? null : open.from;
  1429	  }
  1430	
  1431	  /**
  1432	   * What the snapshot may claim: the pool's prints, its floor, and coverage
  1433	   * from the start of the continuous observed stretch that reaches now (never
  1434	   * before the pool's own window), or from now itself, an empty claim, while
  1435	   * no stream delivers or a hole separates the stream from what was read.
  1436	   */
  1437	  private poolCoverage(session: Session): { prints: readonly TradeFrame[]; floor: number; from: number } {
  1438	    const snapshot = session.pool.snapshot();
  1439	    const nowMs = this.now();
  1440	    const from = this.coverageNowFrom(session);
  1441	    return { prints: snapshot.prints, floor: snapshot.floor, from: from === null ? nowMs : Math.min(nowMs, Math.max(snapshot.from, from)) };
  1442	  }
  1443	
  1444	  /** The runtime's snapshot for the pair as this transport knows it: every closed bar, the last 100 live trades, the newest markers, the print pool. */
  1445	  private snapshotFrame(session: Session): Record<string, unknown> {
  1446	    const bars = [...session.closedBars.values()].sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
  1447	    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
  1448	    const pool = this.poolCoverage(session);
  1449	    return {
  1450	      type: "snapshot",
  1451	      ...identityFields(session.selection),
  1452	      bars: bars.map((row) => barFrame(session.selection, row, true, row.reconstructed)),
  1453	      trades: [...session.recentTrades],
  1454	      markers: markers.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS),
  1455	      vpin_status: "DISABLED",
  1680	  /**
  1681	   * Whether a REST row is tape the stream already delivered. Inside a live
  1682	   * span (open or closed) it is; at a span's first or last millisecond the
  1683	   * aggregate's fill ids decide: wholly before the first live trade or wholly
  1684	   * after the last one it is missing tape and is kept, wholly within it is
  1685	   * dropped, and an aggregate straddling the boundary cannot be told apart,
  1686	   * so it is dropped and that millisecond is marked unverified: no coverage
  1687	   * claim includes it.
  1688	   */
  1689	  private admitRestRow(session: Session, row: AggTradeRow): boolean {
  1690	    const spans: readonly LiveSpan[] = session.live === null
  1691	      ? session.liveSpans
  1692	      : [...session.liveSpans, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, firstId: session.live.firstId, lastId: Infinity }];
  1693	    for (const span of spans) {
  1694	      if (row.T > span.from && row.T < span.to) return false;
  1695	      if (row.T === span.from) {
  1696	        if (row.l < span.firstId) continue;
  1697	        if (row.f >= span.firstId) return false;
  1698	        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
  1699	        return false;
  1700	      }
  1701	      if (row.T === span.to) {
  1702	        if (row.f > span.lastId) continue;
  1703	        if (row.l <= span.lastId) return false;
  1704	        this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
  1705	        return false;
  1706	      }
  1707	    }
  1708	    return true;
  1709	  }
  1710	
  1711	  private markUnverified(session: Session, ms: number): void {
  1712	    if (!session.unverified.includes(ms)) session.unverified.push(ms);
  1713	  }
  1714	
  1715	  /**
  1716	   * One validated page into the retention and the pool, as the runtime's
  1717	   * warm-up seeds a storage chunk: rows from the calibration window (the
  1718	   * newest fifteen minutes at the bootstrap's start) calibrate the floor
  1719	   * (`calibrate: true`, every size counted), older rows are admitted at the
  1720	   * floor; rows the stream already delivered are left out. Returns the rows kept.
  1721	   */
  1722	  private seedTape(session: Session, rows: readonly AggTradeRow[]): number {
  1723	    const calibration: TradeFrame[] = [];
  1724	    const older: TradeFrame[] = [];
  1725	    let oldestTs = Infinity;
  1726	    for (const row of rows) {
  1727	      if (!this.admitRestRow(session, row)) continue;
  1728	      const trade = aggTradeFrame(session.selection, row);
  1729	      if (row.T < oldestTs) oldestTs = row.T;
  1730	      (row.T >= session.calibrationFrom ? calibration : older).push(trade);
  1731	      this.offerMarker(session, trade, false);
  1732	    }
  1733	    if (oldestTs === Infinity) return 0;
  1734	    if (calibration.length > 0) session.pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs: oldestTs, calibrate: true });
  1735	    if (older.length > 0) session.pool.seed(older, { fromMs: oldestTs });
  1736	    return calibration.length + older.length;
  1737	  }
  1738	
  1739	  /** The newest hole between two observed stretches (the open stream span counts) that is neither given up on nor made only of unverified milliseconds. */
  1740	  private nextSeamGap(session: Session): TapeInterval | null {
  1741	    const intervals = this.coverageIntervals(session);
  1742	    for (let i = intervals.length - 1; i >= 1; i -= 1) {
  1743	      const gap: TapeInterval = { from: intervals[i - 1].to, to: intervals[i].from, endAggId: intervals[i - 1].endAggId, endTradeId: intervals[i - 1].endTradeId };
  1744	      if (session.unfillable.some((open) => open.from === gap.from && open.to === gap.to)) continue;
  1745	      const interior = gap.to - gap.from - 1;
  1746	      const unverifiedInside = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms > gap.from && ms < gap.to).length;
  1747	      if (interior <= unverifiedInside) continue;
  1748	      return gap;
  1749	    }
  1750	    return null;
  1751	  }
  1752	
  1753	  /**
  1754	   * Closes the holes the tape has: between the bootstrap's newest row and the
  1755	   * stream's first trade, or between one stream's last trade and the next
  1756	   * stream's first. Kline recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the prints of
  1757	   * such a hole are read from REST within a bounded budget: by id from the
  1758	   * row after the last REST row when the hole follows one, else by time from
  1759	   * the hole's first millisecond, every row reconciled against the live tape
  1760	   * by fill id. A hole that cannot be closed stays a hole and is never inside
  1761	   * a completeness claim. One fill runs at a time; when nothing is left to
  1762	   * fill and a snapshot is waiting, it goes out.
  1763	   */
  1764	  private fillNextSeam(session: Session): void {
  1765	    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || session.seam.inFlight || session.seam.timer !== null) return;
  1766	    const gap = this.nextSeamGap(session);
  1767	    if (gap === null) {
  1768	      if (session.seam.publishWhenSettled) this.publishTapeState(session);
  1769	      return;
  1770	    }
  1771	    void this.fillSeam(session, gap);
  1772	  }
  1773	
  1774	  private async fillSeam(session: Session, gap: TapeInterval): Promise<void> {
  1775	    const seam = session.seam;
  1776	    seam.inFlight = true;
  1777	    seam.gap = gap;
  1778	    let closed = false;
  1779	    let endAggId = gap.endAggId;
  1780	    try {
  1781	      let fromId: number | null = gap.endAggId === undefined ? null : gap.endAggId + 1;
  1782	      for (let pages = 0; pages < MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES; pages += 1) {
  1783	        const rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? { startTime: gap.from } : { fromId }, seam);
  1784	        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
  1785	        const inside = rows.filter((row) => row.T <= gap.to);
  1786	        if (inside.length > 0) {
  1787	          this.seedTape(session, inside);
  1788	          endAggId = inside[inside.length - 1].a;
  1789	        }
  1790	        // The hole is closed when a row past its end was read, or the page
  1791	        // was short (nothing newer existed on the exchange at the read).
  1792	        if (inside.length < rows.length || rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) {
  1793	          closed = true;
  1794	          break;
  1795	        }
  1796	        fromId = rows[rows.length - 1].a + 1;
  1797	      }
  1798	    } catch (error) {
  1799	      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
  1800	      seam.inFlight = false;
  1801	      seam.gap = null;
  1802	      seam.failures += 1;
  1803	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam fill failed", error);
  1804	      if (seam.failures < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP) {
  1805	        const delay = seam.backoffMs;
  1806	        seam.backoffMs = Math.min(seam.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
  1807	        seam.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
  1808	          seam.timer = null;
  1809	          this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1810	        }, delay);
  1811	        return;
  1812	      }
  1813	      seam.failures = 0;
  1814	      seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1815	      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
  1816	      this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1817	      return;
  1818	    }
  1819	    seam.inFlight = false;
  1820	    seam.gap = null;
  1821	    seam.failures = 0;
  1822	    seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1823	    if (closed) {
  1824	      addInterval(session.observed, { from: gap.from, to: gap.to, endAggId });
  1825	      // Coverage grew: the snapshot says so.
  1826	      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
  1827	    } else {
  1828	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam is wider than a fill may walk; it stays open");
  1829	      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
  1830	    }
  1831	    this.fillNextSeam(session);
  1832	  }
  1833	}

    const wanted = this._historyWantedFrom;
    const intervals = this.historyIntervalsAt(minSize);
    const count = intervals.length;
    if (wanted === null || count === 0) return null;
    const newest = intervals[count - 1];

  giveUpHistory(fromMs: number, toMs: number, untilMs: number): void {
    if (!Number.isFinite(fromMs) || !Number.isFinite(toMs) || toMs <= fromMs || !Number.isFinite(untilMs)) return;
    this._givenUp.push({ from: fromMs, to: toMs, until: untilMs });
  }

  askable(ask: HistoryInterval, nowMs: number): HistoryInterval | null {
    this._givenUp = this._givenUp.filter((stretch) => stretch.until > nowMs);
    let to = ask.to;
    for (const stretch of this._givenUp) {
      if (stretch.from < to && stretch.to > ask.from) to = Math.min(to, stretch.from);
    }
    const from = Math.max(ask.from, to - HISTORY_ASK_MAX_MS);
    return to - from < HISTORY_GAP_MIN_MS ? null : { from, to };
  }

  coverHistory(fromMs: number, toMs: number, minSize: number): void {
    if (!Number.isFinite(fromMs) || !Number.isFinite(toMs) || toMs < fromMs || !Number.isFinite(minSize)) return;
    let span: HistoryCoverage = { from: fromMs, to: toMs, floor: minSize };
    const apart: HistoryCoverage[] = [];

      if (entry.floor !== minSize || entry.to < span.from || entry.from > span.to) apart.push(entry);
      else span = { from: Math.min(span.from, entry.from), to: Math.max(span.to, entry.to), floor: minSize };
    }
    apart.push(span);
    apart.sort((a, b) => a.from - b.from || a.floor - b.floor);

    this._coverage = apart;
    this._historyVersion += 1;
    this._notify();
  }

    return () => {
      this._listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  private _notify(): void {
    for (const listener of this._listeners) listener();

  reset(trades: readonly TradeFrame[] = []): void {

    this._lastTs = 0;

  absorb(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): void {
    // A (re)connect: the stretch recorded so far stays complete up to its last live print.
    if (this._liveSince !== null && this._lastLiveTs > this._liveSince) {
      this._liveSpans.push({ from: this._liveSince, to: this._lastLiveTs });
      if (this._liveSpans.length > MAX_LIVE_SPANS) this._liveSpans.splice(0, this._liveSpans.length - MAX_LIVE_SPANS);
    }
    this._liveSince = this._now();
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      const print = toPrint(trade);
      this._push(print);
      this._fold(print);
      taken += 1;
    }
    this._trim();
    if (taken > 0 || this._prints.length === 0) this._notify();
  }

  add(trade: TradeFrame): void {
    if (this._liveSince === null) this._liveSince = this._now();
    if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) return;
    const print = toPrint(trade);
    this._push(print);
    this._fold(print);
    this._trim();
    this._notify();
  }

  addHistory(trades: readonly TradeFrame[], thinBeforeMs = -Infinity): number {
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      this._push(toPrint(trade, true), false);
      taken += 1;
    }
    if (taken === 0) return 0;
    this._prints.sort(comparePrints);

    this._historyVersion += 1;
    this._tallyDirty = true;
    this._settleUntil = this._now() + HISTORY_SETTLE_MS;
    this._notify();
    return taken;
  }

// The tape-derived frames of browser-direct mode: markers from the live tape
// and from the REST bootstrap of recent aggregate trades, the aggressive-print
// pool on the snapshot, aggressive_history answered from the pool, marker_history
// for a client that opted in, and the honest states when the bootstrap fails.
// The bootstrap pages come from the same 10,000 recorded BTC-USDT aggregate
// trades the parity test drives the runtime modules with, so the markers on
// the wire are compared with the runtime's own history buffer here as well.

import path from "node:path";

  BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS,
  BrowserBinanceTransport,

  HEALTH_REASON_READY,

  createHistoryBuffer(options: { markerBucketMs: number; maxMarkersPerBucketSide: number }): {
    recordMarker(marker: MarkerFrame): boolean;
    snapshotMarkers(): MarkerFrame[];

const recorded = loadRecordedTrades();
const FIRST_ID = Number(recorded[0].trade_id.slice(1));
const LAST_ID = Number(recorded[recorded.length - 1].trade_id.slice(1));
const OLDEST_TS = recorded[0].ts_exchange;
const NEWEST_TS = recorded[recorded.length - 1].ts_exchange;
// "now" is a few seconds after the newest recorded trade, inside the forming minute.
const NOW_MS = NEWEST_TS + 5_000;
const MINUTE = Math.floor(NOW_MS / 60_000) * 60_000;
const SPOT = { type: "select", symbol: "BTC-USDT", provider: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT" };

/** The mirror's aggTrades row for a recorded trade. */
function aggTradeRow(trade: TradeFrame): Record<string, unknown> {
  const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
  return { a: id, p: String(trade.price), q: String(trade.size), f: id, l: id, T: trade.ts_exchange, m: trade.side === "sell", M: true };
}

/** Serves a tape as the mirror would: the newest page without parameters, the 1,000 ids from `fromId`, or the first 1,000 rows at or after `startTime`. */
function recordedPage(url: string, rows: readonly TradeFrame[] = recorded): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  const params = new URL(url).searchParams;
  const fromId = params.get("fromId");
  const startTime = params.get("startTime");
  if (startTime !== null) return rows.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange >= Number(startTime)).slice(0, TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE).map(aggTradeRow);
  const first = Number(rows[0].trade_id.slice(1));
  const last = Number(rows[rows.length - 1].trade_id.slice(1));
  const start = fromId === null ? Math.max(first, last - TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE + 1) : Number(fromId);
  return rows.filter((trade) => {
    const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
    return id >= start && id < start + TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE;
  }).map(aggTradeRow);
}

function historyRequest(requestId: number, fromTs: number, toTs: number, minSize: number, cursor?: string) {
  return { type: "aggressive_history", request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromTs, to_ts: toTs, min_size: minSize, ...(cursor === undefined ? {} : { cursor }) };
}

/** Sends one aggressive_history request and returns its reply (or null when the transport drops it). */
async function askHistory(h: Harness, request: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }> | null> {
  const before = h.frames.length;
  h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(request));
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
  const reply = h.parsed().slice(before).find((frame) => frame.type === "aggressive_history");
  return reply === undefined ? null : reply as Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }>;
}

const klineRow = (openTime: number, close: string) => [openTime, "79500.00", "79600.00", "79400.00", close, "5.000", openTime + 59_999, "0", 50, "2.000", "0", "0"];
const klines = [klineRow(MINUTE - 120_000, "79510.00"), klineRow(MINUTE - 60_000, "79520.00"), klineRow(MINUTE, "79530.00")];

class FakeUpstreamSocket implements UpstreamSocket {
  static instances: FakeUpstreamSocket[] = [];
  readyState = 0;
  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
  readonly url: string;
  constructor(url: string) {
    this.url = url;
    FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.push(this);
  }
  close(): void {
    this.readyState = 3;
  }
  open(): void {
    this.readyState = 1;
    this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
  }
  message(payload: unknown): void {
    this.onmessage?.(new MessageEvent("message", { data: JSON.stringify(payload) }));
  }
  drop(): void {
    this.readyState = 3;
    this.onclose?.(new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006 }));
  }
}

type Harness = {
  transport: BrowserBinanceTransport;
  frames: Record<string, unknown>[];
  parsed: () => ServerFrame[];
  aggTradesMock: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
  stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket;
  now: () => number;
};

/** The instance the worker creates after a transport closed: the same mirror, the same clock, the pair's kept tape state. */
function reconnected(previous: Harness): Harness {
  const previousAggTrades = previous.aggTradesMock as unknown as (url: string) => Promise<Response>;
  const next = harness((url) => previousAggTrades(url), previous.now);
  next.aggTradesMock = previous.aggTradesMock;
  return next;
}

/** A hand-built mirror row with explicit fill ids. */
function aggRow(a: number, T: number, f: number, l: number, q = "5.00000"): Record<string, unknown> {
  return { a, p: "79530.00", q, f, l, T, m: false, M: true };
}

function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
}

function harness(aggTrades: (url: string) => Promise<Response> = async (url) => jsonResponse(recordedPage(url)), now: () => number = () => NOW_MS): Harness {
  FakeUpstreamSocket.instances = [];
  const aggTradesMock = vi.fn(aggTrades);
  const transport = new BrowserBinanceTransport("ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws", {
    fetch: ((url: string) => (url.includes("/aggTrades") ? aggTradesMock(url) : Promise.resolve(jsonResponse(klines)))) as unknown as typeof fetch,
    WebSocket: FakeUpstreamSocket,
    now,
  });
  const frames: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  transport.onmessage = (event) => {
    frames.push(JSON.parse(event.data as string) as Record<string, unknown>);
  };
  return {
    transport,
    frames,
    parsed: () => frames.map((frame) => {
      const parsed = parseServerFrame(frame, { nowMs: now() });
      if (parsed === null) throw new Error(`frame rejected by parseServerFrame: ${JSON.stringify(frame).slice(0, 400)}`);
      return parsed;
    }),
    aggTradesMock,
    stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.at(-1)!,
    now,
  };
}

async function openAndSelect(h: Harness, { markerHistory = true } = {}): Promise<void> {
  h.transport.onopen = () => {
    if (markerHistory) h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "capabilities", marker_history: true }));
    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(SPOT));
  };
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
  expect(h.transport.readyState).toBe(1);
}

/** Lets the sequential REST pages of the bootstrap resolve (each page is a few promise hops). */
async function settle(): Promise<void> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i += 1) await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
}

/** Lets a bootstrap read its pages and, with a stream connecting or open but silent, wait out the grace before committing them. */
async function settleBootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  await settle();
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS);
  await settle();
}

function liveTrade(id: number, ts: number, size: string, sell: boolean, price = "79530.00") {
  return { stream: "btcusdt@trade", data: { e: "trade", E: ts + 5, s: "BTCUSDT", t: id, p: price, q: size, T: ts, m: sell, M: true } };
}

function runtimeMarkers(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): MarkerFrame[] {
  const buffer = createHistoryBuffer({ markerBucketMs: MARKER_BUCKET_MS, maxMarkersPerBucketSide: MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE });
  for (const trade of trades) buffer.recordMarker({ ...trade, type: "marker" });
  return buffer.snapshotMarkers();
}
