  vi.useFakeTimers();
  clearBrowserTapeStates();
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("BrowserBinanceTransport tape bootstrap", () => {
  it("reconciles trades sharing a millisecond at both seam boundaries by fill id, and keeps an unverifiable millisecond outside every claim", async () => {
    const C = NEWEST_TS + 3_000;
    const L = C + 1_000;
    const M = C + 5_000;
    const h1 = harness(async (url) => {
      if (url.includes(`fromId=${LAST_ID + 1}`)) {
        // The seam after the pages: one row before the live span; at its first
        // millisecond one wholly before the first live trade (kept), one
        // straddling it (unverifiable) and one wholly after (the stream's).
        return jsonResponse([
          aggRow(LAST_ID + 1, NEWEST_TS + 1_000, 400, 400),
          aggRow(LAST_ID + 2, C, 490, 495),
          aggRow(LAST_ID + 3, C, 498, 500),
          aggRow(LAST_ID + 4, C, 505, 507),
          aggRow(LAST_ID + 5, C + 10, 508, 508),
        ]);
      }
      if (url.includes(`startTime=${L}`)) {
        // The seam after a lost stream whose last trade was id 502 at L: one
        // wholly delivered, one wholly after it (kept), one straddling it.
        return jsonResponse([
          aggRow(9_001, L, 501, 502),
          aggRow(9_002, L, 503, 504),
          aggRow(9_003, L, 502, 503),
          aggRow(9_004, M + 5, 700, 700),
        ]);
      }
      return jsonResponse(recordedPage(url));
    });
    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
    try {
      await openAndSelect(h1);
      await settleBootstrap();
      h1.stream().open();
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
      h1.frames.length = 0;
      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(500, C, SEAM_SIZE, false));
      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(501, C, SEAM_SIZE, true));
      h1.stream().message(liveTrade(502, L, SEAM_SIZE, false));
      await settle();
      const snapshot = h1.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
      if (snapshot === undefined || snapshot.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
      const ids = snapshot.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
      expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 1}`);
      expect(ids).toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 2}`);
      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 3}`);
      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 4}`);
      expect(ids).not.toContain(`a${LAST_ID + 5}`);
      expect(ids).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["500", "501", "502"]));
      // Millisecond C holds a fill nobody can place: coverage reaching now starts after it.
      expect(snapshot.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(C + 1);
      expect(h1.transport.tapeCoverage.unverified).toBe(1);
      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(1, OLDEST_TS, C, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(2, OLDEST_TS, C + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
      expect(await askHistory(h1, historyRequest(3, C + 1, L + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });

      h1.stream().drop();
      const h2 = reconnected(h1);
      await openAndSelect(h2);
      await settle();
      h2.stream().open();
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
      h2.frames.length = 0;
      h2.stream().message(liveTrade(600, M, SEAM_SIZE, false));
      await settle();
      const after = h2.parsed().find((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot");
      if (after === undefined || after.type !== "snapshot") throw new Error("expected a snapshot");
      const afterIds = after.aggressive_prints!.map((print) => print.trade_id);
      expect(afterIds).toContain("a9002");
      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9001");
      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9003");
      expect(afterIds).not.toContain("a9004");
      expect(h2.transport.tapeCoverage.unverified).toBe(2);
      expect(after.aggressive_prints_from).toBe(L + 1);
      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(4, C + 1, L, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(5, C + 1, L + 1, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" });
      expect(await askHistory(h2, historyRequest(6, L + 1, M + 1_000, 1))).toMatchObject({ complete: true });
    } finally {
      warn.mockRestore();
    }
  });

  it("commits the bootstrap pages only once the stream's first trade can be reconciled, so a page that landed first is not counted twice", async () => {
  it("commits the staged pages after a short grace while the stream is connecting or open but silent", async () => {
    const silent = harness();
    await openAndSelect(silent);
    await settle();
    // Every page read while the socket was still connecting: staged.
    expect(silent.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(10);
    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
    silent.stream().open();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS - 1);
    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    await settle();
    expect(silent.frames.filter((frame) => frame.type === "snapshot")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(silent.transport.tapeCoverage.staged).toBe(0);
  });

  it("bounds the coverage bookkeeping across many reconnects and forgets what is past the pool's retention", async () => {
    const clock = { now: NOW_MS };
    const nowFn = () => clock.now;
    let h = harness(async (url) => {
      if (url.includes("startTime")) throw new Error("network");
      return jsonResponse([]);
    }, nowFn);
    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
    try {
      const rounds = 200;
      const stepMs = 2 * 60_000;
      for (let round = 0; round < rounds; round += 1) {
        if (round > 0) h = reconnected(h);
        await openAndSelect(h);
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
        h.stream().open();
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
        h.stream().message(liveTrade(round + 1, clock.now - 1_000, SEAM_SIZE, false));
        // Let the seam fill fail its three reads, then move on.
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS * 4);
        const coverage = h.transport.tapeCoverage;
        expect(coverage.observed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
        expect(coverage.liveSpans).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
        expect(coverage.unfillable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
        clock.now += stepMs;
        h.stream().drop();
      }
      // Six and a half hours of two-minute rounds: only the last four hours remain.
      const h2 = reconnected(h);
      await openAndSelect(h2);
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
      const kept = h2.transport.tapeCoverage;
      const withinRetention = Math.ceil(POOL_WINDOW_MS / stepMs) + 1;
      expect(kept.liveSpans).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
      expect(kept.liveSpans).toBeGreaterThan(withinRetention - 3);
      expect(kept.observed).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
      expect(kept.unfillable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(withinRetention);
      expect(kept.observed).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    } finally {
      warn.mockRestore();
    }
  });
});

import type {
  AggressivePrint,
  AggressiveTape,
} from "../indicators/aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";

/**
 * The terminal's own L1 print store, as an indicator sees it.
 *
 * `AggressiveTape` holds every print of the live pair -- fed the moment it lands
 * -- plus whatever history the server could serve for before this browser
 * connected (a bounded, **floor-filtered** pool, not the raw tape). It is the
 * same store the whale detector and Aggressive Order Flow read.
 *
 * Because history is served above a size floor and the live tape is complete,
 * a window is either covered by every print (`"all"`), covered only by prints
 * above that floor (`"thin"`), or not covered at all (`"none"`). An indicator
 * that prices volume has to say which one it is looking at rather than assume.
 */
export type TickCoverage = "all" | "thin" | "none";

export interface IndicatorTicks {
  /** Every print the store still holds, oldest first. */
  readonly prints: readonly AggressivePrint[];
  /** The instant complete live recording began, or null when nothing is live yet. */
  readonly liveSince: number | null;
  /** How the store covers `[fromTs, toTs)`. */
  coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage;
}

/** The index of the first print at or after `ts`, or `prints.length`. */
export function firstPrintAtOrAfter(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  ts: number,
): number {
  let low = 0;
  let high = prints.length;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
    if (prints[middle].ts < ts) low = middle + 1;
    else high = middle;
  }
  return low;
}

export function sumPrintsIn(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): { volume: number; priceVolume: number; priceSquaredVolume: number } {
  let volume = 0;
  let priceVolume = 0;
  let priceSquaredVolume = 0;
  for (let index = firstPrintAtOrAfter(prints, fromTs); index < prints.length; index += 1) {
    const print = prints[index];
    if (print.ts >= toTs) break;
    const { price, size } = print;
    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0) continue;
    volume += size;
    priceVolume += price * size;
    priceSquaredVolume += price * price * size;
  }
  return { volume, priceVolume, priceSquaredVolume };
}

function coversWindow(
  intervals: readonly { from: number; to: number }[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): boolean {
  return intervals.some(
    (interval) => interval.from <= fromTs && interval.to >= toTs,
  );
}

export function indicatorTicksOf(tape: AggressiveTape | null): IndicatorTicks | null {
  if (tape === null) return null;
  return {
    get prints(): readonly AggressivePrint[] {
      return tape.prints;
    },
    get liveSince(): number | null {
      return tape.liveSince;
    },
    coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage {
      // Every print from the moment live recording began; the window's end may
      // be in the future while the bar is still forming, which is still covered.
      // An earlier live stretch (before a reconnect) covers what lies inside it.
      // Either is complete only past the newest live print the store's size cap
      // dropped: a window it reached lost prints, so it reads as thin.
      const liveFrom = tape.liveSince;
      const live =
        (liveFrom !== null && fromTs >= liveFrom) ||
        tape.liveSpans.some((span) => span.from <= fromTs && span.to >= toTs);
      if (live) return fromTs > tape.liveDroppedThrough ? "all" : "thin";
      // A floor of zero is the server saying it served this stretch in full.
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(0), fromTs, toTs)) return "all";
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(Infinity), fromTs, toTs))
        return "thin";
      return "none";
    },
  };
}

      'import { createAggressivePool, DEFAULT_CALIBRATION_MS, DEFAULT_WINDOW_MS as POOL_WINDOW_MS, type AggressivePool } from "../lib/tape/aggressivePool";\n' +
      'import { MARKER_BUCKET_MS, MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE } from "../lib/tape/largeTradeDetection";\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export const BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE = "browser-binance";\n' +
      'export type DataSource = typeof BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE;\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export const DATA_SOURCE_QUERY_KEY = "source";\n' +
      '\n' +
      '/**\n' +
      ' * The data source for this page: the `?source=` query wins (so a deployed\n' +
      ' * bundle can be switched either way), then the build-time VITE_DATA_SOURCE.\n' +
      ' * Anything but "browser-binance" means the runtime WebSocket, as today.\n' +
      ' */\n' +
      'export function resolveDataSource(envValue: unknown, search: string | undefined): DataSource | undefined {\n' +
      '  let fromQuery: string | null = null;\n' +
      '  if (typeof search === "string" && search.length > 0) {\n' +
      '    try {\n' +
      '      fromQuery = new URLSearchParams(search).get(DATA_SOURCE_QUERY_KEY);\n' +
      '    } catch {\n' +
      '      fromQuery = null;\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '  if (fromQuery !== null) return fromQuery === BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE ? BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE : undefined;\n' +
      '  return envValue === BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE ? BROWSER_BINANCE_SOURCE : undefined;\n' +
      '}\n' +
      '\n' +
      '/** The part of the WebSocket interface marketDataWorker.ts uses. A real WebSocket satisfies it. */\n' +
      'export interface TransportSocket {\n' +
      '  readyState: number;\n' +
      '  send(data: string): void;\n' +
      '  close(): void;\n' +
      '  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null;\n' +
      '}\n' +
      '\n' +
      '/** What this transport needs from the upstream (Binance) WebSocket. */\n' +
      'export interface UpstreamSocket {\n' +
      '  readyState: number;\n' +
      '  close(): void;\n' +
      '  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null;\n' +
      '  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null;\n' +
      '}\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export type BrowserBinanceEndpoints = {\n' +
      '  spotRestBase: string;\n' +
      '  spotStreamBase: string;\n' +
      '  perpetualRestBase: string;\n' +
      '  perpetualStreamBase: string;\n' +
      '};\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export const DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS: Readonly<BrowserBinanceEndpoints> = Object.freeze({\n' +

      '  perpetualRestBase: "https://fapi.binance.com",\n' +
      '  perpetualStreamBase: "wss://fstream.binance.com",\n' +
      '});\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export type BrowserBinanceTransportOptions = {\n' +
      '  fetch?: typeof fetch;\n' +
      '  WebSocket?: new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket;\n' +
      '  now?: () => number;\n' +
      '  setTimeout?: (callback: () => void, ms: number) => unknown;\n' +
      '  clearTimeout?: (handle: unknown) => void;\n' +
      '  endpoints?: Partial<BrowserBinanceEndpoints>;\n' +
      '};\n' +
      '\n' +
      'export const SNAPSHOT_BARS = 900;\n' +
      'export const BAR_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000;\n' +
      'export const REST_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n' +
      'export const STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS = 1_000;\n' +
      'export const STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 30_000;\n' +
      'export const STREAM_FAILURES_BEFORE_UNAVAILABLE = 5;\n' +
      '/** A browser WebSocket has no connect timeout of its own; a proxy that swallows the upgrade would otherwise leave the book "connecting" for good. */\n' +
      'export const STREAM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;\n' +
      "/** Klines per REST page while filling a gap; Binance's maximum. */\n" +
      'export const GAP_FILL_PAGE = 1_000;\n' +
      "/** Pages a gap fill may walk before the history is simply re-read as a fresh snapshot (11,000 minutes covers the 10,080 bars the browser keeps and stays inside the parser's eight-day live window). */\n" +
      'export const MAX_GAP_FILL_PAGES = 11;\n' +
      'export const MAX_GAP_FILL_MS = MAX_GAP_FILL_PAGES * GAP_FILL_PAGE * BAR_INTERVAL_MS;\n' +
      '/** Closed bars held back while a gap fill is pending; older ones are dropped and come back through the paginated REST read instead. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_HELD_CLOSED_BARS = 120;\n' +
      '/**\n' +
      ' * A REST row is taken as final only when its minute closed this long before\n' +
      ' * the request went out: a row served while its minute was still open is\n' +
      " * partial, and the browser's clock may lead the exchange's by a little.\n" +
      ' */\n' +
      'export const CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS = 5_000;\n' +
      '/** Consecutive fills that found the same minute missing before the history is re-read as a snapshot that carries the gap as a gap. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_HOLE_RETRIES = 3;\n' +
      '\n' +
      "/** Aggregate trades per REST page while bootstrapping the tape; Binance's maximum. */\n" +
      'export const TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE = 1_000;\n' +
      '/** Pages the bootstrap walks back from now (weight 4 each: 120 of the 6,000 per minute budget). On BTC-USDT this is minutes of tape, on a quiet pair hours. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGES = 30;\n' +
      '/** Failed attempts (no page read at all) before the READY health frame says the tape history is missing; retries continue. */\n' +
      'export const TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_FAILURES_BEFORE_DEGRADED = 3;\n' +
      '/** Pages a seam fill (the tape between two observed stretches) may walk before the gap is left open. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES = 10;\n' +
      '/** Failed reads of one seam before its gap is left open. */\n' +
      'export const SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP = 3;\n' +
      '/** With a stream open but silent when the bootstrap pages are all read, how long they wait for a first trade before committing without one. */\n' +
      'export const BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS = 2_000;\n' +
      '/** Bounds on the coverage bookkeeping, beyond the retention pruning. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES = 128;\n' +
      'export const MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS = 1_024;\n' +
      '/** Pairs whose tape state (markers, pool, coverage) is kept across transport instances, so a reconnect does not bootstrap again. */\n' +
      'export const MAX_TAPE_STATES = 4;\n' +
      "/** How far back a marker is kept, as the runtime's large-trade history window (BACKFILL_DAYS). */\n" +
      'export const MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n' +
      "/** The runtime's history buffer caps (history.js DEFAULT_MAX_MARKERS, DEFAULT_MAX_TRADES) and the wire's snapshot caps (frames.js). */\n" +
      'export const MAX_RETAINED_MARKERS = 100_000;\n' +
      'export const SNAPSHOT_RECENT_TRADES = 100;\n' +
      'export const MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS = 5_000;\n' +
      'export const MAX_MARKER_HISTORY_ROWS = 5_000;\n' +
      'export const MAX_SNAPSHOT_CLOSED_BARS = 10_080;\n' +
      '/** aggressive_history contract limits (frames.js MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES, aggressive-history.js). */\n' +
      'export const MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_TRADES = 10_000;\n' +

import type { MarkerFrame } from "../../types/frames";
import { markerMagnitude, type WhaleTradeStat } from "../indicators/whale-detector/whaleTrades";

export const DEFAULT_MARKER_COVERAGE_BUCKET_MS = 60_000;
export const DEFAULT_MAX_MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE = 4;
const HEAP_COMPACTION_RATIO = 4;

function lowerMagnitudePriority(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  const candidateMagnitude = markerMagnitude(candidate);
  const incumbentMagnitude = markerMagnitude(incumbent);
  if (candidateMagnitude !== incumbentMagnitude) return candidateMagnitude < incumbentMagnitude;
  if (candidate.ts_exchange !== incumbent.ts_exchange) return candidate.ts_exchange < incumbent.ts_exchange;
  return candidate.trade_id < incumbent.trade_id;
}

function olderFirst(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  return candidate.ts_exchange !== incumbent.ts_exchange
    ? candidate.ts_exchange < incumbent.ts_exchange
    : candidate.trade_id < incumbent.trade_id;
}

function snapshotOrder(a: MarkerFrame, b: MarkerFrame): number {
  return a.ts_exchange - b.ts_exchange || (a.trade_id < b.trade_id ? -1 : a.trade_id > b.trade_id ? 1 : 0);
}

function higherMagnitudeFirst(candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame): boolean {
  return lowerMagnitudePriority(incumbent, candidate);
}

function statOrder(left: WhaleTradeStat, right: WhaleTradeStat): number {
  return snapshotOrder(left.marker, right.marker);
}

type LowerThan = (candidate: MarkerFrame, incumbent: MarkerFrame) => boolean;

function heapSwap(heap: MarkerFrame[], left: number, right: number): void {
  const tmp = heap[left];
  heap[left] = heap[right];
  heap[right] = tmp;
}

function heapPush(heap: MarkerFrame[], value: MarkerFrame, lower: LowerThan): void {
  heap.push(value);
  let index = heap.length - 1;
  while (index > 0) {
    const parent = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
    if (!lower(heap[index], heap[parent])) break;
    heapSwap(heap, index, parent);
    index = parent;
  }
}

function heapSiftDown(heap: MarkerFrame[], index: number, lower: LowerThan): void {
  while (true) {
    const left = index * 2 + 1;
    const right = left + 1;
    let lowest = index;
    if (left < heap.length && lower(heap[left], heap[lowest])) lowest = left;
    if (right < heap.length && lower(heap[right], heap[lowest])) lowest = right;
    if (lowest === index) return;
    heapSwap(heap, index, lowest);
    index = lowest;
  }
}

function heapPopRoot(heap: MarkerFrame[], lower: LowerThan): MarkerFrame | undefined {
  const root = heap[0];
  const last = heap.pop();
  if (heap.length > 0 && last !== undefined) {
    heap[0] = last;
    heapSiftDown(heap, 0, lower);
  }
  return root;
}

  stats: WhaleTradeStat[];

1. **WARNING — `apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1693–1705`**  
   A closed stream span contained within one millisecond has `from === to`. The first-boundary branch handles it first and drops every aggregate with `f >= firstId`, including aggregates wholly **after** `lastId`. Those missing trades are never seeded or marked unverified, yet the seam can become complete.  
   **Fix:** Reconcile against both ID boundaries when timestamps coincide. Add a test with a one-trade span and aggregates before, inside, after, and straddling it.

2. **WARNING — `apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1653–1661`**  
   Bootstrap coverage includes the oldest row’s entire millisecond. A walk stopped at the page budget can start midway through that millisecond; earlier aggregates sharing its timestamp were never fetched. ID continuity within the fetched pages does not establish complete timestamp coverage at that edge.  
   **Fix:** Exclude the oldest millisecond unless its beginning is verified, or fetch the preceding boundary rows. Test a page-budget cutoff splitting several aggregates with the same timestamp.

3. **WARNING — `apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:1516–1519`**  
   History completeness uses the live interval’s infinite endpoint. Requests up to 60 seconds into the future pass validation and can receive `complete: true` with empty trades. This records unobserved time as complete history.  
   **Fix:** Bound history completeness by a finite observation cutoff; return unavailable for windows extending beyond it. Add a future-window regression test.

Executed targeted probes against the branch’s transpiled code: finding 1 dropped the after-span aggregate without marking uncertainty; finding 3 returned complete empty future history. The full test suite was not run; the read-only checkout is on `main`.
